Our success criterion is that constituent layers satisfy the quarter-wavelength condition of the dominant phonon frequencies at a target temperature. This strategy identifies novel bilayer combinations that achieve thermal resistance of up to 3000 times greater than previously reported superlattices. State-of-the-art ab-initio nonadiabatic phonon self-energy relies on an infinite electron lifetime approximation, which cannot capture the temperature dependence of the phonon spectrum, neglects long-wavelength intraband phonon decay, and exhibits exaggerated phonon splitting. In MgB$_2$, we show how the higher-order nonadiabatic phonon corrections mitigate these deficiencies, yielding linewidths in a closer experimental agreement and a dome-like coupling strength temperature dependence.

</details>


### [65] [Data-Efficient Construction of Material-Specific Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials from Ab Initio Molecular Dynamics Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14899)
*Jonas Hänseroth,Christian Dreßler*

TL;DR: 本文系统量化了将通用机器学习原子间势（基础模型）通过微调转化为材料专用势所需的第一性原理数据量，发现2000个AIMD构型是稳健默认值，200个仅在有利情况下有效，10个不足；从头训练在MACE和SevenNet上可与朴素微调竞争甚至略优，且轨迹级误差低不保证反应势垒正确，需观测级验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用或基础机器学习原子间势虽为原子模拟提供了有吸引力的起点，但针对特定材料可观测量的精度常因缺乏额外参考数据（微调）而受限，因此需要量化将通用模型转化为从头算精度材料专用势所需的数据规模，并判断微调是否必然优于从头训练。

Method: 比较五种通用MLIP框架（MACE-MP-0、SevenNet-0、GRACE-1L-OAM、MatterSim-v1-5M、ORB-v2），在七个含稀有和反应事件的化学多样体系上进行微调实验；使用10、200、2000个AIMD导出的构型，并测试对AIMD轨迹进行中等密度子采样以缩短轨迹长度；同时对比从头训练，并采用多模型平均来改善稀缺数据下的预测。

Result: 10个AIMD构型微调不足；200个在有利体系成功但强依赖体系；2000个构型产生低力/能量误差并复现目标观测量。中等密度子采样可十倍缩短所需轨迹长度且质量损失很小。从头训练在MACE和SevenNet上与朴素微调相当或略优，GRACE需更多数据。MoS2硫空位跳变能垒显示低轨迹级误差不保证正确反应轮廓。多模型平均在稀缺数据下无额外DFT成本提升预测。

Conclusion: 有限的AIMD参考数据可转化为可靠的材料专用MLIP，实现近DFT精度的纳秒时间尺度模拟；建议2000个构型为默认数据量，需进行观测级验证而非仅看误差指标，且微调并非总是优于从头训练，模型平均是低成本提升策略。

Abstract: Pretrained machine-learning interatomic potentials, so-called universal or foundation models offer an appealing starting point for atomistic simulations, but their accuracy for material-specific observables often remains limited without additional reference data (fine-tuning). Here, we systematically quantify how much first-principles data are required to convert universal models into ab initio-accurate material-specific potentials, and ask whether fine-tuning is necessarily preferable to training from scratch. We compare five universal MLIP frameworks, MACE-MP-0, SevenNet-0, GRACE-1L-OAM, MatterSim-v1-5M and ORB-v2, across seven chemically diverse systems incorporating rare and reactive events. Fine-tuning on only 10 AIMD-derived configurations is insufficient for the investigated systems; 200 configurations succeed in favorable cases, but the outcome remains strongly system-dependent. By contrast, 2000 AIMD configurations constitute a robust default, yielding low force and energy errors and reproducing the target material-specific observables. Moderately dense sub-sampling of the AIMD trajectory reduces the required trajectory length tenfold with little loss in model quality. Training from scratch on the same datasets is competitive with, and often slightly more accurate than, naive fine-tuning for MACE and SevenNet, whereas GRACE requires more data. The energy profile for a sulfur-vacancy jump in MoS$_2$ reveals that low trajectory-level errors do not guarantee a correct reaction profile, highlighting the need for observable-level validation. Finally, we show that averaging independently trained models improves predictions in scarce-data regimes at no additional first-principles cost. Together, these results provide practical guidelines for converting limited AIMD reference data into reliable material-specific MLIPs for nanosecond-timescale simulations at near-DFT accuracy.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cu₂O最低黄系paraexciton（Γ₂⁺）在电偶极和电四极近似下都是光学禁戒的，传统光谱无法直接激发；作者希望探索携带轨道角动量的光场能否突破这一对称性限制，从而为研究该重要激子态提供新途径。

Method: 采用微观对称性分析，识别与paraexciton耦合的允许OAM通道（梯度辅助l=5和直接l=6），并进一步对有限束腰的Laguerre–Gaussian光束及局域OAM场计算矩阵元；通过强度环半径与激子玻尔半径的对比，建立光学与激子长度尺度之间的定量关系，并分析目标三次谐波多项式阶数对最优局域化的影响。

Result: 对称性分析得到l=5和l=6两个主导OAM通道，但普通高斯-拉盖尔光束因空间变化远慢于激子半径而严重抑制耦合；只有局域OAM场才能满足“空间变化尺度接近激子”的条件。对Γ₂⁺ paraexciton（六次谐波），最大耦合出现在强度环半径约6a_B–7a_B处。

Conclusion: paraexciton激发由对称性和空间局域化共同决定：立方对称性筛选允许的OAM通道，多项式阶数决定高效耦合的特征径向尺度。该工作为在Cu₂O中用结构光激发paraexciton提供了对称性框架和实用设计规则。

Abstract: In Cu$_2$O the lowest yellow exciton, the $Γ_2^+$ paraexciton, is optically inaccessible in conventional spectroscopy because transitions to this state are forbidden in both electric-dipole and electric-quadrupole approximations. We investigate whether optical fields carrying orbital angular momentum (OAM) can overcome this restriction. A microscopic symmetry analysis identifies the gradient-assisted $l=5$ and direct $l=6$ OAM channels as the leading contributions that couple to the paraexciton, independent of the detailed radial profile of the optical field. Calculations for finite-waist Laguerre--Gaussian beams, however, show that the corresponding matrix elements are strongly suppressed because the optical field varies only weakly over the exciton Bohr radius. Thus, satisfying the OAM selection rule alone is insufficient: efficient excitation requires not only the correct angular symmetry but also optical-field variations on the spatial scale of the exciton. This second condition is achieved by localized OAM fields. Expressing the coupling in terms of the physical intensity-ring radius provides a direct comparison between the optical and excitonic length scales and reveals that the optimal localization is determined primarily by the polynomial degree of the target cubic harmonic. For the degree-six $Γ_2^+$ paraexciton the strongest coupling occurs for an intensity-ring radius of approximately $6a_B$--$7a_B$. These results establish that paraexciton excitation is governed jointly by symmetry and spatial localization: cubic symmetry selects the allowed OAM channels, whereas the polynomial degree sets the characteristic radial scale for efficient coupling. This work provides both the symmetry framework and a practical design rule for engineering structured-light excitation of paraexcitons in Cu$_2$O.

</details>


### [67] [Probing three-dimensional structures of complex colloidal quantum dots at the single-atomic level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14962)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该工作创下原子级表征新基准，建立了从三维结构到电子性质的全链条分析框架，为下一代纳米材料的理性设计提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Colloidal quantum dots (QDs) are promising optoelectronic materials due to their size-tunable properties, yet their three-dimensional (3D) quantum confinement makes electronic states highly sensitive to structural and chemical heterogeneity, which critically impacts their optoelectronic performance. Accurately resolving the 3D atomic structure with sub-angstrom precision is thus essential for rational design. Here, we applied atomic electron tomography (AET) to determine, for the first time, the 3D atomic structure of complex core/shell QDs, resolving over 14,000 atoms per particle. Our reconstructions reveal surface morphology, eccentric cores, and nearly atomically abrupt heterovalent interfaces and identify anisotropic shell growth directed by twin boundaries. Utilizing an AET-derived atomic structure, we performed large-scale quantum mechanical calculations to uncover an orientation-dependent strain accommodation mechanism where the heterogeneous strain is compensated at interfaces and twin boundaries. Furthermore, our results reveal strain-induced localized states near the band edge, which contribute to the key features of the experimental ensemble absorption spectrum. This work sets a new benchmark for atomic-level characterization, establishing a powerful framework for the rational design of next-generation nanomaterials.

</details>


### [68] [Discovering Physically Interpretable Mathematical Expression for Predicting CO2 Adsorption in Metal-Organic Frameworks via Machine Learning-Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14990)
*Yimin Shao,Shengluo Ma,Shenghong Ju,Yijun Shi,Wei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种机器学习-符号回归（ML-SR）策略，从少量数据中推导出可解释的CO2吸附容量公式Q=aA，并在大规模hMOF验证中取得良好预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑箱ML模型缺乏物理解释性，难以揭示吸附机制；需要一种既能高效预测又具备物理可解释性的表达式。

Abstract: This work presents a machine learning-symbolic regression (ML-SR) strategy to develop a physically interpretable formula for predicting low pressure CO2 adsorption capacity in hypothetical metal-organic frameworks (hMOFs). Four ML models were trained on a small dataset of 1,000 samples, and five key descriptors-largest cavity diameter, pore limiting diameter, void fraction, gravimetric surface area, and number of hydrogen atoms-were identified through SHAP and feature importance analyses. Symbolic regression was then employed to derive a concise adsorption formula, Q=aA, where a represents an adsorption baseline (mmol/g) and A is a dimensionless adsorption number incorporating four structural descriptors. We interpret A as the ratio between an adsorption binding force and a diffusion driving force, revealing how pore topology and surface chemistry jointly influence adsorption. Validation against a comprehensive dataset of 137,652 hMOFs demonstrates that this formula achieves over 70% prediction accuracy for 62,448 structures, confirming strong applicability within defined structural and operational ranges. Unlike conventional black box ML models, the proposed physics-guided expression enables efficient prediction and provides clearer insight into adsorption mechanisms.

Abstract: Moiré superlattices reshape lattice dynamics without altering chemical composition, yet how crystal anisotropy modifies this control remains unclear. At 300 K, twisting suppresses the band-path heat-capacity-weighted mean-square group velocity to 2.6--8.4\% of the control values; the suppression spans a broad frequency range rather than a few soft branches. The velocity response crosses over between $4.78^\circ$ and $3.82^\circ$ into a regime where the relaxed stacking textures and frequency-resolved velocity profiles become self-similar, with the normalized mean-square velocity ratio spanning only 11.1\% of its mean across the three smallest angles---a finite anisotropic crossover, not a singular-angle condition. Direct DFT--MACE force-constant agreement ($r=0.996$), uniform $4\times4\times1$ stability scans, and acoustic-sum-rule and path-density tests support the trend. The equilibrium trend is defined by six structures after excluding one relaxation-sensitive case. These results extend phonon twistronics to low-symmetry layered materials and identify crystal anisotropy as a key determinant of finite-angle phonon crossover behavior.

</details>


### [70] [Network Topology of Hafnia-Based Amorphous Optical Coatings by Grazing-Incidence X-ray Total Scattering and Atomic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15011)

Abstract: Amorphous hafnia-based films are promising optical-coating materials for cryogenic GW detectors, but their performance depends on how doping and annealing modify the atomic network. We combine grazing-incidence X-ray total scattering measurements with experimentally constrained atomic modeling to study the as-deposited HfO$_2$ and 27% SiO$_2$-doped HfO$_2$ films annealed at 150$^\circ$C and 400$^\circ$C. Pure amorphous HfO$_2$ is a dense, high-coordination network of Hf-centered polyhedra with substantial edge- and face-sharing connectivity. Incorporating SiO$_2$ introduces stable SiO$_4$ tetrahedra, lowers the Hf and O coordination, and replaces highly connected Hf-rich oxygen environments with mixed Si--O--Hf bridges. This produces a chemically mixed network rather than isolated SiO$_2$-rich regions, and shifts the cation topology toward corner-sharing connectivity. Annealing to 400$^\circ$C produces only modest additional structural relaxation. These results provide an atomic-scale description of how SiO$_2$ modifies the topology of amorphous HfO$_2$-based coatings and suggest structural descriptors relevant to understanding their mechanical-loss behavior.

</details>


### [71] [DINO4DSTEM: A self-supervised framework for structural discovery in 4D-STEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15098)
*Daniel Khaykelson,Lothar Houben,Boris Rybtchinski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: DINO4DSTEM 是一个自监督机器学习框架，无需结构模型、人工标注或预定义类别，即可直接从 4D-STEM 衍射数据中自动发现结构状态，实现复杂材料的定量纳米尺度结构映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D-STEM 纳米衍射虽已成为定量结构映射的关键技术，但高维数据集在结构复杂材料中的解析仍是瓶颈；现有工作流依赖结构模型、手动注释、预定义类别或样本特定启发式方法，限制了对异质复杂材料的表征能力。

Method: 引入 DINO4DSTEM，采用自监督表示学习，直接从原始衍射数据中学习按内在结构相似性组织的表征，无需结构模型、人工标签、预定义类别数或系统特定参数调优，将大量低剂量测量转化为定量纳米尺度结构图。

Result: 在多个数据集上一致识别出主导结构自由度，实现无监督分割；应用于吲哚美辛（一种束敏感、多域药物体系）的结晶过程，揭示结晶性从部分有序前驱体出现并跨越连续的结构有序谱，所发现的纳米尺度结构状态被映射以定量展示样品中有序性的演化。

Conclusion: DINO4DSTEM 用通用自监督表示学习替代任务特定分析，为复杂材料的定量纳米尺度结构映射提供了广泛适用的框架，能够发现异质、束敏感体系中涌现的结构组织。

Abstract: Nanodiffraction using 4D-STEM has become a key technique for quantitative nanoscale structural mapping in materials research, yet interpreting its high-dimensional datasets in structurally complex materials remains a major bottleneck. Existing analysis workflows typically rely on structural models, manual annotation, predefined classes, or sample-specific heuristics, limiting their ability to characterize heterogeneous complex materials. Here, we introduce DINO4DSTEM, a self-supervised machine learning framework that automatically discovers structurally meaningful states directly from raw diffraction data. Without structural models, manual labels, a predefined number of classes, or system-specific parameter tuning, the framework learns representations that organize diffraction patterns by their intrinsic structural similarities, transforming large collections of low-dose measurements into quantitative nanoscale structure maps. Across diverse datasets, DINO4DSTEM consistently identifies the dominant structural degrees of freedom, providing segmentation without human supervision. We applied the framework to the crystallization of indomethacin, a beam-sensitive, multidomain pharmaceutical system, revealing that crystallinity emerges from a partially ordered precursor and spans a continuous spectrum of structural order. The discovered nanoscale structural states are mapped to reveal the evolution of order across the specimen quantitatively. By replacing task-specific analysis with general self-supervised representation learning, DINO4DSTEM provides a broadly applicable framework for quantitative nanoscale structural mapping in complex materials, enabling the discovery of emergent structural organization in heterogeneous, beam-sensitive systems.

</details>


### [72] [Dimensionality Mismatch Enables Decoupled Heat and Charge Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16261)
*Luman Shang,Shuming Zeng,Chenhan Liu,Yu Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过高吞吐筛选发现准一维材料中晶格与电子输运维度失配可实现热与电荷输运解耦，SbTeI在900K附近获得约2.1的zT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热电材料中热导率和电导率相互关联，解耦热与电荷输运是关键挑战，传统策略难以突破。

Method: 对Materials Project数据库进行高吞吐筛选，识别具有强链内-弱链间键合层级结构的准一维材料，并对比其晶格热输运与载流子输运的各向异性。

Result: Sn2S3和SbTeI等材料中，声子沿链传播而横向热导被抑制，但横向价带态提供有效链间空穴耦合，形成热-电输运倒置各向异性；筛选体系中链间晶格热导显著降低，而空穴输运弱各向异性甚至偏向链间方向，SbTeI最大zT约2.1（900K）。

Conclusion: 维度失配可作为解耦声子与载流子输运的通用策略，为设计高性能热电材料提供新方向。

Abstract: Decoupling heat and charge transport is a key challenge in thermoelectrics. Here, we identify a route to spatially separate phonon and carrier transport in quasi-one-dimensional materials through high-throughput screening of the Materials Project database. Representative Sn$_2$S$_3$ and SbTeI exhibit a strong-intrachain--weak-interchain bonding hierarchy that favors phonon propagation along the chains while suppressing transverse lattice heat transport. In contrast, transverse valence-band states provide effective interchain electronic coupling and relatively light hole transport. This mismatch between lattice and electronic transport dimensionalities produces an inverted thermal--electrical anisotropy. Across the screened candidates, interchain lattice thermal conductivity is strongly suppressed, whereas hole transport remains weakly anisotropic or even favors the interchain direction. For SbTeI, this decoupling yields a maximum $zT$ of approximately 2.1 near 900~K. These results establish dimensionality mismatch as a general strategy for decoupling phonon and carrier transport in thermoelectric materials.

</details>


### [73] [Linking Electronic Bonding and Short-range Order to Strength in $α$-Titanium Alloys: A First-Principles Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15227)
*Md Faiz Akhtar,Nilesh P. Gurao,Somnath Bhowmick*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过DFT计算与量子化学描述符（ICOHP）建立α-Ti合金抗拉强度预测模型，揭示合金元素对电子键合与短程有序的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钛合金强度预测对先进材料设计至关重要，传统经验模型缺乏物理基础，需发展基于电子键合的定量预测方法。

Method: 利用密度泛函理论（DFT）系统研究替代式（Al, V, Mo）与间隙式（H, C, N, O）合金元素的作用，分析短程有序（SRO）及电子键合，采用Integrated Crystal Orbital Hamilton Population (ICOHP) 作为键强描述符构建强度预测公式。

Result: 所得公式能定量预测多种α-Ti合金的抗拉强度，验证了方法的有效性与广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作突破了传统经验模型的局限，为高性能结构材料的计算设计提供了显著进展。

Abstract: The development of accurate strength prediction models for titanium alloys is critical for advanced materials design. This study systematically examines how the mechanical properties of $α$-Ti are affected by substitutional (X = Al, V, Mo) and interstitial (Y = H, C, N, O) alloying elements, with a focus on electronic bonding. Using density functional theory (DFT), we uncover the short-range ordering (SRO) of substitutional atoms and quantify their influence on the electronic bonding and mechanical behavior. The primary novelty of this work lies in developing a predictive model for tensile strength that goes beyond traditional empirical approaches. To quantify the contributions of individual solutes to strengthening, we use physically grounded quantum-chemical descriptors, such as the Integrated Crystal Orbital Hamilton Population (ICOHP), which is a direct measure of bond strength derived from first-principles calculations. The resulting formula quantitatively predicts the tensile strength of a wide range of $α$-Ti alloys, demonstrating a significant advancement in the computational design of high-performance structural materials.

</details>


### [74] [Claude-SpinDynamics: a cross-platform, dual-precision CPU/GPU micromagnetic simulator with native mumax3 script compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15253)
*Chun-Yeol You,Jaeyong Cho*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 介绍 Claude-SpinDynamics (Claude-SD)，一个开源微磁模拟器，支持无 GPU 的 CPU 完整构建，同时提供 GPU 单/双精度、多种 FFT 后端及本征自旋轨道等力矩，兼容 mumax3 脚本，性能与精度均达到或超越现有主流代码，并支持 GPU 副本批处理加速有限温度系综模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定量自旋电子学依赖少数 GPU 微磁代码，这些代码均要求 NVIDIA 硬件且仅支持单精度，导致没有该硬件的研究者无法运行甚至标准验证问题。

Method: 开发了跨平台 C++20 核心（Windows/Linux）与 Python 接口的 Claude-SD；CPU 完整构建通过 GPU 路径同一测试套件；GPU 构建支持单/双精度、两种去磁 FFT 后端，本征实现自旋轨道、自旋转移、Zhang-Li 力矩、DMI 和逐格材料；原生解释 mumax3 的 .mx3 脚本；实现 GPU 副本批处理扩展，单次内核启动推进整个有限温度轨迹系综。

Result: CPU 构建无需加速器即可运行所有单元测试和 uMAG 标准问题；在匹配条件下与 mumax3 逐格一致到单精度舍入误差；在 uMAG 动态开关标准问题上与 mumax+、OOMMF 差异在 2% 以内；单精度构建在小规模和二维问题上最快，最大网格下仍有竞争力；双精度和双 FFT 后端路径在 GPU 微磁代码中独一无二；批处理扩展相比逐轨迹循环吞吐量提升一至两个数量级，并保持数值舍入精度。

Conclusion: Claude-SD 填补了微磁模拟的硬件可及性和精度空白，提供开放源码、文档和示例，支持独立复现，是现有 GPU 微磁代码的有力替代或补充。

Abstract: Quantitative spintronics increasingly depends on a handful of GPU micromagnetic codes, all of which require NVIDIA hardware and single precision throughout, leaving researchers without such hardware unable to run even the standard validation problems. We report Claude-SpinDynamics (Claude-SD), a new open source micromagnetic simulator with a cross platform C++20 core (Windows and Linux) and a Python interface that closes this gap: a complete CPU build, validated by the same test suite as the GPU path, runs every unit test and uMAG standard problem with no accelerator at all, alongside GPU builds offering both single and double precision, a choice of two demagnetization FFT backends, and natively implemented spin-orbit, spin-transfer, and Zhang-Li torques, Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, and percell materials.Claude-SD natively interprets mumax3's .mx3 scripting language, so existing community scripts run unmodified; under matched conditions the two codes agree cell-by cell to single-precision round-off, and, together with mumax+ and OOMMF, to within 2% on the uMAG dynamic-switching standard problem, with MuMax-CO agreeing to mumax3 to float32 round-off on the same problem. Benchmarked head-to-head against these three codes, Claude-SD's single-precision build is the fastest solver on small and two-dimensional problems and remains competitive at the largest grid sizes, while its double-precision and dual-FFT-backend paths are unmatched among GPU micromagnetic codes. A GPU replicabatching extension further advances an entire ensemble of finite-temperature trajectories in a single kernel launch per step, giving one to two orders of magnitude of throughput over a per-trial loop while reproducing single-trajectory results to numerical round-off. The complete source is openly licensed and distributed with runnable example notebooks and documentation for independent reproduction.

</details>


### [75] [Barium Hexaferrite Thin Films as a Scalable Magnetic-Insulator Platform for Proximity-Engineered Spintronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15331)
*Shyam Sundar Poriah,Sanjana D. S.,Agrim Sharma,Sreelakshmi M. Nair,Pankaj Bhardwaj,Laxmipriya Nanda,Aryaman Das,Jagadish Rajendran,R. S. Patel,Manish Jain,Dhavala Suri*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出溅射生长的钡铁氧体（BaFe12O19, BaM）薄膜作为磁性绝缘体平台，其具有无需应变工程的本征垂直磁各向异性，可与Pt和拓扑绝缘体BSTS形成界面并诱导异常霍尔效应，为自旋电子学器件提供了超越石榴石材料的新选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土铁石榴石（如YIG、TmIG）是自旋电子学和磁振子器件的基准磁性绝缘体，但实现可用的垂直磁各向异性（PMA）通常依赖衬底应变工程，需要精确的晶格匹配和特定生长条件，限制了材料可及性。因此需要寻找无需应变工程即可获得强本征PMA的替代磁性绝缘体。

Method: 采用溅射法生长钡铁氧体（BaM）薄膜，通过X射线衍射、透射电子显微镜和拉曼光谱确认薄膜的化学计量比和原子级平滑表面；利用第一性原理计算验证其亚铁磁基态；将BaM分别与Pt和剥离的BiSbTeSe2（BSTS）构筑异质结，测量其输运性质以表征界面交换耦合和自旋纹理。

Result: BaM薄膜具有本征垂直各向异性，无需应变工程，呈现方形面外磁滞回线，矫顽场接近0.1 T。与Pt和BSTS界面接触后，均观测到邻近效应诱导的异常霍尔输运，表明高效的界面交换耦合；BSTS/BaM异质结还出现额外的霍尔贡献，暗示存在非共线界面自旋纹理。

Conclusion: BaM薄膜作为一种可扩展的磁性绝缘体平台，具备强本征PMA、原子级平滑表面和良好的界面耦合特性，适用于自旋电子学和拓扑异质结器件，突破了石榴石化学体系的限制。

Abstract: Rare-earth iron garnets, such as yttrium iron garnet (YIG) and thulium iron garnet (TmIG), are the benchmark magnetic insulators for spintronic and magnonic devices, but achieving usable perpendicular magnetic anisotropy (PMA) in these materials typically relies on substrate strain- engineering, requiring careful lattice-matching and specific growth conditions that constrain ma- terial accessibility. Here we establish sputter grown barium hexaferrite (BaFe12O19, BaM) as a magnetic-insulator alternative with strong intrinsic perpendicular anisotropy, requiring no strain engineering. X-ray diffraction, transmission electron microscopy and Raman spectroscopy confirm stoichiometric films with atomically smooth surfaces, while first-principles calculations corroborate a robust ferrimagnetic ground state. The films exhibit square out-of-plane hysteresis with a coercive field of nearly 0.1 T. Unlike rare-earth garnets, the perpendicular anisotropy in BaM is intrinsic to its magnetoplumbite crystal structure, arising independent of highly ordered strain. Interfaced with Pt and with exfoliated BiSbTeSe2 (BSTS), BaM induces proximity induced anomalous Hall trans- port, confirming efficient interfacial exchange coupling, while the BSTS/BaM heterostructure shows an additional Hall contribution suggestive of non-collinear interfacial spin textures. These results position BaM thin films as a scalable magnetic-insulator platform for spintronic and topological heterostructure devices beyond the constraints of garnet chemistry.

</details>


### [76] [What makes a useful molecular model of biochar? A community roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15348)
*Valentina Sierra-Jimenez,Jonathan P. Mathews,Luca Bellucci,Edo Boek,Carla de Tomas,Manuel Garcia-Perez,Stef Ghysels,Paola Giudicianni,Corinna Maria Grottola,Kelly Anne Hawboldt,Robert L. Johnson,Fenna B. E. Kolff,Jean-Marc Leyssale,Diego Liberati,Francisco J. Martin-Martinez,Jacob W. Martin,Ondřej Mašek,Mohammad Mezbah Ul Hoque,Audrey Ngambia,Amaël Obliger,Frederik Ossler,Muhammad Riaz,John M. Tobin,Xiaolei Zhang,Valentina Erastova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 这篇社区路线图评估了生物炭分子建模的现状，指出第一代模型在足够长度尺度和明确微孔/化学控制下已相当成功，并提出了七个未解决问题和五个社区优先事项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物炭应用广泛，但对其无序碳结构的建模问题独特，没有单一模型能回答所有问题。模型实用性需针对特定问题并经过实验验证。CECAM研讨会旨在梳理当前方法并确定差距。

Method: 对比了实验引导的自上而下重构、模拟驱动的自下而上模拟和混合方法，强调对微孔性和体相化学的明确控制，以及多尺度工作流中经典力场与反应性方法的结合。

Result: 结构性和平衡界面性质可用经典力场处理，动态/反应行为需反应性方法。矿物/灰分和土壤老化过程是未充分解决的空白。提出七个当前无法可靠回答的问题及五个优先事项。

Conclusion: 需加强实验-模型互动，建立开放模型/数据仓库和标准，并借鉴煤、干酪根、黏土有机质的可迁移方法，以加速进展。

Abstract: Biochars are disordered carbonaceous materials produced by biomass pyrolysis, with applications spanning soil amendment, water remediation, carbon storage, and functional materials. Although they share structural features with other disordered carbons such as coal, kerogen, and activated carbons, the questions posed to biochar models are distinct, and no single model can answer all of them equally well. Model usefulness must be defined relative to a specific question and validated against independent experimental observables. This community roadmap, arising from a CECAM workshop, critically maps current molecular approaches: experimentally guided top-down reconstruction, mimetic bottom-up simulation, and hybrid methods. We argue that first-generation models have been more successful than is often acknowledged, provided they are built at sufficient length scale and with explicit control over microporosity and bulk chemistry. Structural and equilibrium interfacial properties are increasingly tractable with classical force fields, whereas dynamic and reactive behaviours require selective use of reactive methods within multiscale workflows. A parallel, largely unaddressed gap concerns the mineral and ash components of biochar, and the changes the material undergoes during ageing in soil. We identify seven open questions current models cannot yet answer reliably, and five community priorities: force field benchmarking, open model and data repositories, shared classification and metadata standards, ensemble validation, and training in reproducible practice. Across these, sustained interaction with experimentalists is essential to ground models in real observables and document where they fail. Progress will be accelerated by adapting transferable methods from coal, kerogen, and clay-organic matter frameworks rather than repeating trial-and-error development.

</details>


### [77] [Intrinsic Wannier Functions for Hamiltonian downfolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15557)
*Shuoxue Li,Garnet Kin-Lic Chan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出了一种称为内禀Wannier函数（IWF）的新方法，用于将材料的第一性原理能带结构降折叠到由指定原子轨道构成的低能子空间，该方法非迭代、仅需一个无量纲参数即可实现能带解缠，并在硅、石墨烯及汞铜氧化物三带模型中验证了其高质量与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wannier降折叠是模拟复杂材料时的常见任务，但现有方法（如最大局域化Wannier函数和密度矩阵选定列方法）通常需要迭代优化或复杂参数调整，缺乏简单、稳健且适用于高通量计算的通用方案。

Method: IWF方法直接基于给定原子特征构造内禀Wannier函数，非迭代实现，仅需一个无量纲参数即可完成能带解缠，从而避免繁琐的迭代过程与多参数调优。

Result: 在硅、石墨烯和汞铜氧化物三带模型的基准测试中，IWF方法生成的降折叠能带结构与标准方法（MLWF、SCDM）相当，且实现简单、鲁棒性更好。

Conclusion: IWF方法具有通用性和实用性，适合作为材料电子结构研究和高通量应用中Wannier降折叠的标准工具。

Abstract: Downfolding ab initio material band structure into a low-energy subspace spanned by orbitals of specified atomic character, a procedure known as Wannier downfolding, is a common task in the simulation of complex materials. We introduce the Intrinsic Wannier Function (IWF) method to Wannierize bands with given atomic character. The method is non-iterative and requires only a single dimensionless parameter to disentangle bands. In benchmarks on silicon, graphene, and the three-band model of a mercury cuprate, we show that Intrinsic Wannier Functions provide high quality downfolded band structures compared to those from standard approaches such as Maximally Localized Wannier Functions and the Selected Columns of the Density Matrix method. Further, their straightforward implementation and robustness positions Intrinsic Wannier Functions as a general and useful tool for Wannier downfolding in materials electronic structure and in high-throughput applications.

</details>


### [78] [Intrinsic Defects in Amorphous Optical Coatings of TiO$_2$-doped GeO$_2$ for Gravitational-wave Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15386)
*K. Prasai,K. Lee,R. Bassiri,A. Davenport,D. A. Drabold,M. M. Fejer,A. Markosyan,C. S. Menoni,S. Tait*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理模拟和吸收测量，发现TiO₂掺杂GeO₂薄膜的残余吸收源于富钛环境中的氧空位，这些缺陷形成Ti³⁺-Ti³⁺极化子对或混合Ti极化子态，其跃迁吸收1064 nm光，而非由非晶网络的普通结构无序导致；因此该吸收并非本征限制，可通过控制氧化学计量比来抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来引力波探测器需要镜面涂层的光吸收低于每镜0.1 ppm，而目前最佳高折射率材料TiO₂掺杂GeO₂即使在消除外部污染后仍存在ppm级吸收，需要明确其残余吸收的微观起源以进一步降低热噪声。

Method: 结合从头算（ab-initio）模拟和光热吸收测量，分析非晶网络中的结构无序与氧空位缺陷的电子结构，并对比干/惰性退火前后的吸收变化以验证缺陷的形成机制。

Result: 普通结构无序虽能局域电子态却不产生吸收1064 nm光的缺陷；而致密富钛环境中的氧空位形成局域化Ti³⁺-Ti³⁺极化子对或混合Ti极化子态，其跃迁能量接近1064 nm。光热测量显示干/惰性退火后吸收增加，支持形成这类还原敏感缺陷。

Conclusion: TiO₂掺杂GeO₂的残余吸收不是材料本征限制，而是与工艺相关的缺陷，可通过在沉积和退火过程中控制氧化学计量比来减轻。

Abstract: The increased laser power of future gravitational-wave detectors will require mirror coatings with optical absorption below 0.1 ppm per mirror. TiO$_2$-doped GeO$_2$, currently the best high-index material for reducing room-temperature coating thermal noise, still exhibits ppm-level absorption even after extrinsic contamination is minimized. Within experimental uncertainty, the activation barriers remain comparable, while the prefactors are reduced significantly for D, indicating isotope-dependent attempt frequencies and zero-point energy effects. TDS simulations based on the Polanyi-Wigner formalism reproduce the experimental desorption spectra by resolving subsurface and near-interface contributions, in agreement with the NRA profile. Abstract: Point defects in atomically thin materials have a strong impact on physical properties and those that induce in-gap states are advantageous for quantum information science and engineering (QISE). However, dopant engineering consisting of well-controlled synthesis and robust identification of in-gap states is challenging. In this work, we addressed this challenge by first using finely tuned chemical vapor deposition to incorporate vanadium dopants into a monolayer WS2 (V-WS2). The latter included conductive atomic force microscopy (cAFM), low temperature scanning tunneling microscopy and spectroscopy (STM/STS), and scanning transmission electron microscopy and unambiguously revealed that vanadium dopants form deep in-gap states 0.35 eV above the valence band maximum in V-WS2. Our experimental results are well supported by first principles calculations and taken together demonstrate that V-WS2 is a promising platform for QISE applications.

### [81] [Design of altermagnetism in oxide superlattices exploiting interface effects and quantum confinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15765)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过理论计算提出在氧化物超晶格中利用对称性破缺、静电掺杂和限域效应，将交替磁性（altermagnetism）与金属性结合。在(SrCrO3)1/(SrTiO3)1(001)中获得高达350 meV的非相对论自旋劈裂，并在(SrCrO3)4/(LaCrO3)4(001)中实现金属性d波交替磁体，自旋劈裂达120 meV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交替磁体多为绝缘块体材料，缺乏金属性体系，阻碍其输运与自旋电子学应用。为此需要设计策略在氧化物超晶格中实现交替磁性与金属性的共存。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论+Hubbard U（DFT+U）计算，通过构建(SrCrO3)1/(SrTiO3)1(001)和SrCrO3/LaCrO3(001)超晶格，分析对称性破缺、静电掺杂、限域效应及八面体旋转对电子结构和磁性的影响。

Result: 单层SrCrO3受限在(SrCrO3)1/(SrTiO3)1(001)中因轨道有序与八面体旋转共存产生高达350 meV的d波非相对论自旋劈裂；在(SrCrO3)4/(LaCrO3)4(001)中，界面极性不连续和更强的八面体旋转促使其成为金属性d波交替磁体，界面Cr d带在费米能级附近贡献120 meV自旋劈裂，并出现自旋选择性费米面嵌套迹象。

Conclusion: 氧化物超晶格可作为实现和探索交替磁性的新兴平台，有望用于量子输运与自旋电子学功能器件。

Abstract: The discovery of altermagnetism has initiated intensive research and opened new avenues for spin- tronic and transport applications. While current efforts are mostly focused on bulk materials which are typically insulating, here we propose design strategies to achieve a combination of altermag- netism and metallicity in oxide superlattices by exploiting symmetry breaking, electrostatic doping and confinement. While bulk SrCrO3 does not exhibit altermagnetism due to compensating effects between adjacent layers our density functional theory calculations with a Hubbard U parameter re- veal, that a single SrCrO3 layer confined in a (SrCrO3)1/(SrTiO3)1(001) superlattice (SL) exhibits a sizable non-relativistic spin splitting (NRSS) up to 350 meV with bulk d-wave nature due to the coexistence of orbital ordering and octahedral rotations (OORs). Since this system is insulating, we extend to SrCrO3/LaCrO3(001) SLs. In the (SrCrO3)4/(LaCrO3)4(001) SL the combination of a polar discontinuity at the interface and stronger OORs promotes metallic d-wave altermagnetism. The NRSS of up to 120 meV is contributed by the interfacial Cr d bands at the Fermi level with indications for a spin-selective Fermi surface nesting. These findings establish oxide superlattices as a promising platform to realize and explore altermagnetism for quantum transport and spintronic functionalities

</details>


### [82] [Graph neural network prediction of temperature-dependent hydrogen diffusion and thermal conductivity tensors of tungsten containing helium bubbles and grain boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15609)
*S. Saito,M. I. Kobayashi,T. Kasahara*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一个图神经网络代理模型，可基于钨的原子构型（含氦泡和晶界）在毫秒级输出氢扩散系数张量和热导率张量，并通过Arrhenius与Wiedemann-Franz-Matthiessen关系解析外推到任意温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氦泡和晶界会数量级地改变钨中氢同位素输运和热传导，但用传统分子动力学逐构型评估耗时数小时，无法支撑组件尺度聚变偏滤器的微结构分辨输运模拟。

Method: 采用旋转等变的张量池化层捕获各向异性，预测与温度无关的物理参数（Arrhenius对、声子热导张量、缺陷剩余电阻率），再解析展开到目标温度。训练标签来自635种微结构的EAM势分子动力学模拟（Green-Kubo热导率与多温度示踪扩散），电子热导通道用辐照退化实验校准，并通过FLARE/VASP主动学习流程锚定第一性原理精度。

Result: 学习到的活化能中位数0.21 eV，随泡/晶界结构升高，与文献中氢迁移势垒和捕获物理吻合；等变池化保证任意取向子块预测一致。耦合有限元分析表明，热导率退化通过温度场使氢渗透预测改变2.5倍；单次推理仅需毫秒。

Conclusion: 该代理模型能够快速提供微结构分辨的完整输运张量，为聚变装置钨部件提供多尺度仿真输入，弥合原子尺度计算与组件尺度热量/氢输运分析的鸿沟。

Abstract: Helium bubbles and grain boundaries in tungsten plasma-facing components alter hydrogen-isotope transport and thermal conduction by orders of magnitude, yet evaluating these transport properties for a given microstructure requires hours of molecular dynamics (MD) per configuration. We present a graph neural network surrogate that maps a tungsten atomic configuration containing helium bubbles and grain boundaries directly to the full $3\times3$ symmetric tensors of the hydrogen diffusion coefficient $D_H(T)$ and the thermal conductivity $κ(T)$ at arbitrary temperature. Anisotropy is captured by a rotation-equivariant tensor pooling layer; temperature enters through predicted temperature-independent parameters (an Arrhenius pair $(D_0,E_a)$, a phonon conductivity tensor, and a defect residual resistivity) expanded analytically via the Arrhenius and Wiedemann-Franz-Matthiessen relations. Training labels for 635 microstructures are generated with an embedded-atom-method potential (Green-Kubo conductivity and multi-temperature tracer diffusion); the electronic channel is calibrated against published irradiation-degradation measurements, and the pipeline is anchored to a first-principles machine-learning potential (VASP+FLARE) through paired MD calibration runs and an active-learning loop. The learned activation energies (median 0.21 eV, rising in bubble and grain-boundary structures) reproduce literature hydrogen migration barriers and trapping physics, and the equivariant pooling keeps predictions consistent across arbitrarily oriented sub-blocks. Coupled finite-element thermal-hydrogen analyses driven by the surrogate show that conductivity degradation changes predicted hydrogen permeation by a factor of 2.5 through the temperature field. The model returns both tensors in milliseconds, enabling microstructure-resolved transport input for component-scale analyses of fusion divertors.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 明确了配体空穴在驱动Ag2BiO3键歧化绝缘态中的关键作用，并将该材料确立为有前景的自旋电子学候选材料。

Abstract: The origin of the proposed bond disproportionated insulating state of the non-centrosymmetric ($Pnn2$) phase of Ag$_{2}$BiO$_{3}$ is explored using first principles electronic structure calculations. The novel insulating state is elucidated by first considering the initially proposed centosymmetric metallic ($Pnna$) phase of Ag$_{2}$BiO$_{3}$. Our calculations reveal that the valence skipping Bi$^{4+}$ ions in this phase are better described as Bi$^{3+}\underline{L}$ with completely filled Bi-(6$s$) states and a ligand hole. However, phonon calculations indicate that the metallic ($Pnna$) state is dynamically unstable. Structural stability is achieved through breathing distortions of the oxygen octahedra, resulting in two inequivalent Bi sites and a reduction of symmetry to the $Pnn2$ phase. Electronic structure calculations further reveal that the $Pnn2$ phase is a bond disproportionated insulator where the nominal charge state of Bi is described by : 2[Bi$^{3+}\underline{L}$ (Bi$^{4+}$)] $\rightarrow$ Bi$^{3+}\underline{L}^{2-δ}$ (Bi1$^{5+}$) + Bi$^{3+}\underline{L}^δ$ (Bi2$^{3+}$), highlighting the crucial role of ligand holes in driving the insulating state. Next we have investigated the electronic structure of Ag$_{2}$BiO$_{3}$ in the insulating ($Pnn2$) phase including spin-orbit coupling. Our density functional theory (DFT ) calculations complemented by ${\bf k.p}$ model Hamiltonian analysis reveal persistent spin-textures around the $X$ and $Y$ high symmetry points of the orthorhombic Brillouin zone imposed by non-symmorphic symmetry, positioning Ag$_{2}$BiO$_{3}$ as a promising candidate for spintronic applications.

</details>


### [84] [Uncovering the deformation mechanism of glasses during indentation through high-resolution X-ray scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15675)
*M. Faizal Ussama Jalaludeen,Søren S. Sørensen,Johan F. S. Christensen,Anders K. R. Christensen,Sidsel Mulvad Johansen,Samraj Mollick,Yuanzheng Yue,Sharafat Ali,Sebastian Kalbfleisch,Morten M. Smedskjaer*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压痕实验可模拟玻璃的日常损伤，但传统研究多关注卸载后的表面形变，难以建立表面形变与结构之间的关联。亚表面变形（含弹性区和塑性区）对理解变形机制至关重要，然而现有表征方法极少，因此需要开发原位手段来探测压痕过程中的亚表面变形。

Method: 使用同步辐射X射线纳米散射技术，在压痕过程中原位探测四种具有不同结构特征的氧化物和氮氧化物玻璃。通过测量X射线结构因子第一尖锐衍射峰的位置和强度变化，实现约100纳米的高空间分辨率表征。

Conclusion: 工作为氧化物玻璃的力学行为提供了新见解，有助于设计更抗损伤的玻璃材料。

Abstract: Indentation experiments can be used to mimic real-life damage events of glasses that lead to surface flaws and thus lower practical strength. Conventional indentation studies often focus on the surface deformation after unloading. However, to understand the link between the surface deformation and structure, it is crucial to characterize the sub-surface deformation during the indentation process. The indentation-induced deformation, consisting of both elastic and plastic zones, is governed by the glass composition and structure, indentation and atmospheric conditions, and stress state. However, only a few experimental methods exist for characterizing the sub-surface indentation deformation mechanism during indentation. In this study, we use synchrotron X-ray nanoscattering to probe the deformation mechanism in situ during indentation of four types of oxide and oxynitride glasses with distinct structural features. This is done by measuring the variation in the position and intensity of the first sharp diffraction peak of the X-ray structure factor with a high spatial resolution down to ~100 nm. We find that the deformation zones of these glasses, which are characterized by the shape, size, and relative contribution between densification and shear flow under different indentation loads, vary with Poisson's ratio. Thus, our work provides new insights into the mechanical behavior of oxide glasses, contributing to the design of more damage-resistant glasses.

</details>


### [85] [ALKEMIE Agent: an autonomous platform for computational materials design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15776)
*Hongfu Huang,Yuzhe Li,Ao Xu,Bo Liu,Changrui Wang,Kan Tang,Ning Yang,Shengxian Liu,Hanyu Liu,Pengpeng Zhang,Linggang Zhu,Fengkai Liu,Yichen Lu,Tong Zhao,Naihua Miao,Jian Zhou,Zhimei Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: ALKEMIE Agent 是一个自主计算框架，通过集成检索增强生成、知识库、注册技能、可追溯控制回路等组件，协调材料计算工作流，减少人工干预，并展示了从材料推荐到主动学习筛选等多种应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料计算工作流碎片化且高度依赖人工，需要不断衔接软件工具、数据分析和中间决策，方法能力与实际执行之间的差距日益扩大，亟需一种能统一协调工具、知识和流程的自主计算框架。

Method: 构建 ALKEMIE Agent，采用检索增强生成、材料计算知识库、注册技能、数据库支持的数据溯源、AI辅助结构建模、有界任务执行、工具调用迭代和错误诊断辅助，并将这些集成在可追踪的控制回路中，实现工作流的统一与自适应。

Result: 在材料推荐、结构建模、声子计算、机器学习势训练、LAMMPS模拟、从头算蒙特卡洛采样和主动学习材料筛选等应用中展示了其能力。

Conclusion: ALKEMIE Agent 为计算材料设计提供了一种自主化平台范式，未来需进一步发展此类智能体平台以应对更多挑战。

Abstract: Despite the powerful multi-scale modeling methods and high-throughput infrastructures established in the materials community, real material computation workflows remain fragmented and heavily manual, requiring researchers to constantly bridge software tools, data analysis, and intermediate decisions. This growing gap between methodological capability and practical execution highlights the need for a new kind of autonomous computational framework, one that can coordinate tools, knowledge, and workflows in a more unified and adaptive way. Here, we introduce ALKEMIE Agent, an agentic platform in which retrieval-augmented generation, a materials-computation knowledge base, registered skills, database-supported provenance, AI-assisted structure modeling, bounded task execution, tool-calling iteration, and error-diagnostic assistance are integrated within a traceable control loop. The capabilities of ALKEMIE Agent are demonstrated through applications including materials recommendation, structure modeling, phonon calculations, machine-learned interatomic potential training, LAMMPS simulations, Ab Initio Monte Carlo (AIMC) sampling, and active-learning-based materials screening. Finally, we outline the future directions and challenges for the development of agentic platforms for computational materials design.

</details>


### [86] [Strain-stabilized altermagnetism and conductivity anisotropy in FeSb2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15839)
