  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索交错磁体（altermagnet）这一新型磁性材料，并寻找可实验检测的输运特征，为自旋电子学器件提供应变可调的候选平台。

Method: 利用密度泛函理论（DFT）和Wannier插值费米面，计算FeSb2在不同应变下的电子结构、自旋劈裂、费米面几何及磁输运各向异性。

Result: FeSb2在拉伸应变下从反铁磁转变为交错磁，近费米能级自旋劈裂约0.2 eV（无自旋轨道耦合），单轴电导各向异性约60%，远超典型铁磁金属。

Conclusion: FeSb2是应变可调的交错磁平台，其独特的磁输运行为可作为交错磁相变的指示，并可用于自旋电子器件。

Abstract: We show that FeSb2 experiences a transition from a conventional antiferromagnet to an altermagnet when tensile strain is applied. In the altermagnetic phase, the lifted Kramers degeneracy results in spin splitting up to ~0.2eV near the Fermi level even without spin-orbit coupling. The transition to the altermagnetic phase is accompanied by a dramatic change in the Fermi-surface geometry, which leads to a uniaxial conductivity anisotropy up to ~60%, much greater than those observed in typical ferromagnetic metals. Using density-functional theory and Wannier interpolated Fermi surfaces, we show that this magnetotransport behavior may function as an experimental indicator of the transition to the altermagnetic phase. These findings highlight FeSb2 as a versatile, strain-tunable platform for exploring and utilizing altermagnetic transport phenomena for spintronic devices.

</details>


### [87] [Rare-earth chalcogenide perovskites: A promising class of materials for optoelectronic applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15882)
*Surajit Adhikari,Priya Johari*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算，系统研究了稀土硫族钙钛矿ABX3（A=Y,La; B=Sc,Y; X=S,Se）的结构稳定性、光电性质、激子动力学与极化子效应，发现其兼具稳定结构、可调带隙、强激子效应和有利的载流子-声子耦合，是前景广阔的无铅光电器件材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土硫族钙钛矿因无毒、相稳定和优异光电性质而受关注，但激子与极化子性质因高精度理论计算成本过高而缺乏系统研究。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论结合多体微扰理论（G0W0与Bethe-Salpeter方程），对系列ABX3化合物进行结构稳定性、准粒子带隙、光吸收、激子结合能及Fröhlich模型载流子-声子耦合分析。

Result: 所有化合物满足动力学与力学稳定判据；准粒子带隙为2.75–4.47 eV；BSE显示可见至紫外强吸收；激子结合能0.148–0.517 eV，激子中等局域化且电子-空穴波函数重叠强；电子-声子耦合为中等至强，且通常电子-声子作用强于空穴-声子作用。

Conclusion: 稀土硫族钙钛矿ABX3综合了结构稳定性、可调光电性能、显著激子效应和有利极化子输运，适合用于发光器件和光电探测器等下一代无铅光电器件。

Abstract: Rare-earth chalcogenide perovskites have attracted significant attention for optoelectronic applications due to their nontoxic composition, robust phase stability, and excellent optoelectronic properties. However, their excitonic and polaronic properties remain largely unexplored due to the high computational cost of accurate theoretical treatments. In this work, we present a comprehensive first-principles investigation of excitonic dynamics and polaronic effects in a series of III-III rare-earth chalcogenide perovskites ABX$_{3}$ (A = Y, La; B = Sc, Y; X = S, Se), along with their structural stability and optoelectronic properties, using state-of-the-art density functional theory in conjunction with many-body perturbation theory within the G$_{0}$W$_{0}$ and Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) frameworks. All investigated compounds satisfy the dynamical and mechanical stability criteria. They exhibit quasiparticle band gaps in the range of 2.75$-$4.47 eV, and the BSE calculations reveal strong optical absorption spanning the visible to ultraviolet regions. The computed excitonic properties indicate intermediate-to-large exciton binding energies (0.148$-$0.517 eV), moderately localized excitons, and strong electron-hole wavefunction overlap, indicative of favorable radiative recombination characteristics and enhanced light-matter interaction. Furthermore, analysis based on the Fröhlich model demonstrates intermediate-to-strong carrier-phonon coupling, with electron-phonon interactions generally stronger than hole-phonon interactions. Overall, rare-earth chalcogenide perovskites ABX$_{3}$ exhibit a compelling combination of structural stability, tunable optoelectronic properties, pronounced excitonic effects, and favorable polaronic transport, positioning them as promising lead-free materials for next-generation optoelectronic devices, including light-emitting devices and photodetectors.

</details>


### [88] [Crystal-structure design by agentic AI in a language of motifs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15900)
*Dinh-Khiet Le,Minh-Quyet Ha,Hong-Phuc Vu-Dinh,Takashi Miyake,Hiori Kino,Hieu-Chi Dam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出MatEvolve，一个基于智能体AI的晶体设计框架，通过可解释的“母题画像”（motif profile）书写和编辑晶体结构，结合第一性原理验证，在稀土贫乏永磁体设计中比生成模型更频繁地发现新结构原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的材料发现在插值上比外推更可靠，且很少能达到新结构类型，限制了真正创新的材料设计。

Method: MatEvolve利用智能体AI，以可解释的“母题语言”将晶体表示为由重复几何图案组成的母题画像；智能体编辑该画像并据此构建晶体，再用第一性原理对最有希望的候选材料进行验证；基于Claude Fable 5语言模型，无需微调。

Result: 在相同验证预算下，MatEvolve发现新结构原型的频率是生成模型的三倍以上，同时达到可比的定向磁性材料命中率；分析所发现晶体的可读画像还能揭示结构-性能关系。

Conclusion: MatEvolve证明智能体AI与可解释结构表示结合，能有效探索新材料结构原型并辅助理解构效关系，为数据驱动材料发现提供新范式。

Abstract: Data-driven materials discovery interpolates more reliably than it extrapolates and seldom reaches new structure types. We present MatEvolve, an agentic-AI framework designing crystals, proposing each candidate with a stated rationale and testing it. The agent reasons in an interpretable \emph{language of motifs}, writing each crystal as a \emph{motif profile} that describes the recurring geometric patterns---the \emph{motifs}---composing it. The motif profile serves not merely as a description of a material but as the medium for material design: the agent edits the profile and constructs a crystal from the modified one, and the most promising candidates are validated by first-principles calculation. Applied to the design of rare-earth-lean permanent magnets, MatEvolve---built on the state-of-the-art language model Claude Fable~5 without fine-tuning---reaches new structural prototypes more than three times as often as generative models under an equal validation budget, at a comparable on-target-magnet rate. Beyond design, analysing the discovered crystals' human-readable profiles reveals structure--property relationships.

</details>


### [89] [Synthesizing like a chemist: an iterative, feedback-driven loop for materials discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15928)
*Fang Sheng,Steven B. Torrisi,Amanda Volk,Kevin Tran,Koki Nakano,Brian W. Anthony,Tonio Buonassisi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种闭环自动化框架，通过大语言模型引入人类隐性知识，结合高光谱成像和多目标贝叶斯优化，实现了从文献知识提取到薄膜合成优化的全自动流程，并成功合成了此前未见报道的钙钛矿衍生化合物Rb3BiI6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合成优化过程缓慢、依赖专家经验且需反复迭代，导致大多数计算预测的材料从未被实际合成，缺乏自动化加速手段。

Method: 构建闭环框架：以大语言模型从文献中提炼合成知识作为初始化；采用高光谱成像快速评估薄膜；利用多目标贝叶斯优化根据实验反馈迭代指导实验，并在一对配对优化实验中与拉丁超立方采样基线对比。

Result: LLM辅助初始化在相同试验次数下产生了更多Pareto最优样本和更高超体积，优势在迭代优化中持续保持；成功合成出新型化合物Rb3Bi6薄膜，并通过光学带隙分析和X射线衍射验证了优化薄膜。

Conclusion: 该框架将合成预测从单次推荐转变为迭代学习，为自动化和全自主实验材料发现提供了一种通用策略。

Abstract: Most computationally predicted materials are never synthesized because conventional synthesis optimization is slow, expertise-dependent, and iterative. Here we present a closed-loop framework that automates this expert workflow by placing human tacit knowledge in the loop through a large language model (LLM) that distills synthesis knowledge from the literature, high-throughput hyperspectral imaging for rapid film evaluation, and multi-objective Bayesian optimization guided by experimental feedback. In a paired optimization campaign, LLM-assisted initialization produced more Pareto-optimal samples and higher hypervolume than a Latin hypercube sampling baseline at matched trial counts, and this advantage persisted throughout iterative optimization. We demonstrate the framework by synthesizing the previously unreported perovskite-inspired compound Rb3BiI6 as thin films and validating the optimized films by optical bandgap analysis and X-ray diffraction. The framework transforms synthesis prediction from single-shot recommendation to iterative learning, providing a generalizable strategy to accelerate automated and fully autonomous experimental materials discovery.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond Local Berry Geometry: A First-Principles Finite-Momentum Theory of Electronic Position](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16020)
*M. S. Si,Y. Q. Li,G. P. Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种有限动量下电子位置的第一性原理理论，保留不同动量间的相干信息，并引入‘相干偶极’概念；在硅中该理论预测了材料动量尺度，并揭示高次谐波重组由动量空间几何而非仅光子能量决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berry框架仅在局域动量空间描述电子态变化，但强场下电子会跨越有限动量范围，此时不同动量间的相干性成为响应的一部分，而现有理论缺失这一信息。

Method: 直接从材料波函数构造有限动量下的电子位置矩阵，无需模型能带或拟合跃迁矩阵元；通过对空间平均中不因相干对消而消失的部分进行刻画，定义相干偶极，从而建立有限动量几何的第一性原理描述。

Result: 在硅中识别出由有限动量几何决定的材料动量尺度；将该尺度与场驱动动量变化对比即可判断有限动量物理是否激活。跨越该尺度后，第五及更高次谐波显著重组，表明非线性响应受控于动量空间几何，而非仅由发射光子能量决定。

Conclusion: 该理论将量子几何扩展到局域Berry极限之外，为真实材料中电场驱动现象提供普遍理论基础；HHG是首个验证场景，但方法适用于任何驱动电子跨越有限动量范围的过程。

Abstract: Electronic position controls how a crystal polarizes and responds to an external field. In crystals, it is usually described through local changes of electronic states in momentum space. This Berry framework has reshaped modern solid-state physics, but strong fields drive electrons across a finite momentum range, where coherence between different momenta becomes part of the response. Here we establish a first-principles theory of electronic position at finite momentum that retains this missing information. We show that unequal-momentum coherence can cancel under spatial averaging and still produce polarization, forming a coherence dipole. We obtain the matrix directly from material wave functions, without model bands or fitted transition elements. In Si, the finite-momentum geometry sets a material momentum scale. Comparing this scale with the momentum change driven by the field predicts when finite-momentum physics becomes active. Crossing the scale strongly reorganizes the fifth and higher harmonics, showing that momentum-space geometry, rather than emitted photon energy alone, controls the nonlinear response. HHG is the first demonstration, but the theory applies whenever driven electrons explore a finite momentum range. It therefore extends quantum geometry beyond the local Berry limit and provides a general basis for predicting field-driven phenomena in real materials.

</details>


### [91] [First-Principles Prediction of Phonon-Mediated Infrared Optical Properties of WO$_3$ Polymorphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16113)
*Sreerag Sundaram,Karthik Sasihithlu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 以三氧化钨（WO₃）为代表性体系，采用全第一性原理方法研究其三种温度相关晶相的中红外光学性质，发现不同相之间存在显著差异，表明该方法可预测材料多晶型对中红外光学行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多晶体具有多晶型现象，原子重排导致晶胞对称性变化，进而改变晶格振动模式和中红外光学性质。理解这种结构-光学关系对材料发现与定向设计至关重要，因此需要系统研究。

Method: 选取WO₃作为模型材料，因其具有多种温度依赖的晶相；采用完全基于第一性原理的计算方法，分别构建并评估三种晶相的中红外光学性质，从而比较不同对称性下的振动和光学响应。

Result: 三种WO₃晶相在中红外光学特性上表现出显著差异，证明晶相多态性会强烈调制中红外光谱响应，且第一性原理方法能够有效捕捉这些差异。

Conclusion: 该第一性原理方法可作为预测性工具，用于材料发现和定向设计，尤其是针对多晶型体系的中红外光学性能调控。

Abstract: Many crystals exhibit polymorphism, undergoing atomic rearrangements that result in unit cells belonging to different symmetry groups. These structural changes directly affect lattice vibration modes and consequently influence their mid-infrared optical properties. In this study, we use tungsten trioxide (WO$_3$) as a representative system, owing to its multiple temperature-dependent phases, to study the impact of polymorphism on mid-infrared optical behaviour. Using a fully first-principles approach, we investigate three phases of WO$_3$ and evaluate their mid-infrared optical properties. Significant differences are observed among the three crystallographic phases, demonstrating the potential of this methodology as a predictive tool for materials discovery and targeted design.

</details>


### [92] [Spin-pump-induced spin transport demonstration in a photoconductive PTCDA molecular thin film with a transparent spin current detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16124)
*Yuri Matsukawa,Eiji Shikoh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在ITO/PTCDA/Ni80Fe20三层结构中，通过Ni80Fe20的铁磁共振自旋泵浦在PTCDA分子薄膜中产生纯自旋流，并被ITO层通过逆自旋霍尔效应转化为电荷流进行检测，同时研究了光照对PTCDA中自旋输运的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索有机分子半导体PTCDA中的自旋输运特性，并利用透明ITO作为自旋流探测器，研究光照对其自旋输运的调制作用，以拓展有机自旋电子学的应用。

Method: 构建ITO/PTCDA/Ni80Fe20三层堆叠样品，利用Ni80Fe20层的铁磁共振驱动自旋泵浦，在PTCDA层中产生纯自旋流；自旋流注入ITO层后经逆自旋霍尔效应转换为电荷流，通过ITO电阻以电动势形式检测；并对比有无光照条件下的自旋输运行为。

Result: 成功在PTCDA薄膜中实现了自旋泵浦诱导的自旋输运，并在ITO层检测到由逆自旋霍尔效应产生的电动势；光照对PTCDA中的自旋输运具有影响。

Conclusion: 证实了PTCDA可作为有机自旋输运层，ITO可用作自旋流检测器，且光照能够调控该有机体系中的自旋输运，为有机自旋电子学器件提供了新方案。

Abstract: We demonstrate spin-pump-induced spin transport in a photoconductive PTCDA (3,4,9,10-perylene-teracarboxylic dianhydride) molecular thin film with a transparent ITO (In2O3 + SnO2) film as a spin current detector. In a tri-layer stacking structure sample composed of ITO/PTCDA/Ni80Fe20, pure spin current is generated in the PTCDA layer by using the spin pumping driven by the ferromagnetic resonance of the Ni80Fe20 layer. The generated spin current is absorbed into the ITO layer, converted to a charge current due to the inverse spin-Hall effect of the ITO layer, and detected as an electromotive force via the ITO resistance. Also, the light irradiation effect on spin transport in PTCDA films is investigated.

</details>


### [93] [Efficient nonequilibrium electron dynamics from first-principles: leveraging Koopmans spectral functionals and Wannier localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16226)
*Giovanni Cistaro,Miguel Sá,Davide Sangalli,Antonio Picón,Nicola Colonna*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的第一性原理方法，用于模拟扩展体系中超出线性响应的非平衡电子动力学，通过结合Koopmans泛函和Wannier基组中的实时密度矩阵演化（HSEX近似），并利用密度泛函微扰论高效计算屏蔽库仑相互作用，能够以较低成本精确描述激子效应对非线性光谱（如高次谐波产生）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时格林函数方法计算成本高，难以在固体中高效模拟包含激子效应的非平衡电子动力学和非线性光学过程，需要一种兼具准确性和可行性的第一性原理框架。

Method: 将Koopmans兼容泛函（提供准确准粒子带结构）与Wannier基组中电子密度矩阵的实时演化相结合，采用Hartree加屏蔽交换（HSEX）近似；利用轨道基的局域性引入物理近似降低计算与内存开销，并通过密度泛函微扰论高效计算HSEX自能中的屏蔽库仑相互作用。

Result: 线性区基准测试与实验光谱及格林函数参考结果吻合，涵盖弱和强束缚激子体系；非线性区对硅和氟化锂的高次谐波产生（HHG）模拟显示，硅的HHG主要由准粒子带结构决定，而LiF中谐波发射在激子共振处选择性增强，表明HHG可探测相关电子-空穴激发而非仅准粒子带结构。

Conclusion: 该框架能以显著低于实时格林函数方法的计算成本实现固体非线性光谱中激子效应的全从头模拟，为研究超快和强场现象提供高效途径，并揭示强关联体系中HHG与激子态的联系。

Abstract: We present an efficient first-principles approach for simulating the nonequilibrium electron dynamics in extended systems beyond the linear regime. The method combines Koopmans-compliant functionals, which provide an accurate quasiparticle band structures, with the real-time evolution of the electronic density matrix in a Wannier basis within the Hartree plus screened exchange (HSEX) approximation. The locality of the orbital basis enables physically motivated approximations that significantly reduce both the computational cost and memory requirements while preserving accuracy. The screened Coulomb interaction, the central ingredient of the HSEX self-energy, is computed efficiently using density-functional perturbation theory. We benchmark the approach in the linear regime against experimental spectra and reference Green's function calculations for systems featuring both weakly and strongly bound excitons. Moving to the nonlinear regime, we investigate high-harmonic generation (HHG) in silicon and lithium fluoride. While in silicon the HHG spectrum is largely governed by the quasiparticle band structure, in LiF, a material featuring strong excitonics effect, the harmonic emission is selectively enhanced at excitonic resonances, suggesting that HHG probes correlated electron-hole excitations rather than solely the quasiparticle band structure. The present framework enables fully \textit{ab-initio} simulations of excitonic effects in nonlinear optical spectra at a significantly reduced computational cost compared to real-time Green's function approaches, providing an efficient route to the study of ultrafast and strong-field phenomena in solids.

</details>


### [94] [Toward the Ultimate Limit: Elemental Metals in One Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16232)
*Mohammad Bagheri,Kameyab Raza Abidi,Sushree Sarita Sahoo,Sukhbir Singh,Pekka Koskinen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过密度泛函理论模拟系统研究了40种非磁性元素金属一维原子链的几何结构、能量、弹性与电子性质，发现多数链呈屈曲基态，Cd、Hg、Sr为半导体，过渡金属保留较高体相内聚能，26种链在100 K下热力学稳定，并揭示选定稳定链的拉直动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维材料性能优异，但以往研究集中于二维层状材料；实验技术已逼近一维原子链极限，亟需系统理论指导以加速一维金属链的合成与表征。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论模拟计算40种非磁性元素金属一维原子链的几何、能量、弹性与电子结构；通过分子动力学评估动力学稳定性；开展链拉伸模拟研究屈曲链的拉直行为。

Result: 几乎所有链具有屈曲基态，9条链发生畸变；Cd、Hg、Sr呈现电子带隙半导体特性；过渡金属在一维链中保留可观的三维体相内聚能；26条链在100 K下热力学稳定；链拉伸模拟揭示了选定稳定链的拉直动力学过程。

Conclusion: 该系统性研究为一维金属原子链提供了全面理论数据库与设计准则，有助于指导实验合成和表征，推动维度降低后新兴物性的探索与应用。

Abstract: Low-dimensional materials exhibit extraordinary properties that make them promising candidates for advanced technologies. Although they have been investigated extensively, most of the research has focused on layered two-dimensional (2D) materials. Here, inspired by recent advances in atomically thin metallenes, we further reduce dimensionality and use density-functional theory simulations to study the geometry, energetics, elasticity, and electronic structure of 40 non-magnetic one-dimensional (1D) atomic chains of elemental metals. We find that nearly all chains have a buckled ground state, nine chains are distorted, and three chains---Cd, Hg, and Sr---are semiconducting with an electronic gap. We also find that transition metals retain a substantial fraction of their 3D bulk cohesive energy even in 1D chains. We assessed chains' dynamical stabilities by molecular dynamics simulations and found that 26 of them are thermodynamically stable at 100 K. Finally, we performed chain pulling simulations to investigate the straightening dynamics of selected stable chains. Given that experimental techniques have recently reached the 1D-chain limit, our systematic study provides a foundation and timely guide to accelerating synthesis and characterization of these materials.

</details>


### [95] [Effect of Magnetic Vacancies on the Spontaneous Spin-Reorientation Transition in HoFe$_{1-x}$Al$_x$O$_3$ Single Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16348)
*S. A. Skorobogatov,V. A. Babkina,M. S. Pavlovskii,M. I. Kolkov,S. V. Semenov,I. N. Khoroshiy,S. E. Nikitin,K. A. Shaykhutdinov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 首次生长出Al取代的HoFe₁₋ₓAlₓO₃单晶（x≤0.2），发现Al掺杂通过引入磁空位改变Ho³⁺离子的各向异性平衡，从而拓宽Γ₁₂相在自旋重取向温度区间的稳定范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HoFeO₃具有独特的Γ₄-Γ₂₄-Γ₁₂-Γ₂自旋重取向序列，该序列由Ho³⁺的K_ac与K_ab各向异性竞争及Fe³⁺弱铁磁有效场共同决定。研究Al掺杂如何通过磁空位扰动Fe³⁺亚晶格，进而调控SRT行为，对理解磁各向异性竞争机制具有重要意义。

Method: 生长HoFe₁₋ₓAlₓO₃（x≤0.2）单晶，通过Al³⁺取代Fe³⁺引入磁空位，系统研究Al浓度对自发自旋重取向转变的影响，结合磁相图分析各向异性变化。

Result: Al掺杂产生的额外有效磁场作用于Ho³⁺离子，改变了SRT温区内K_ac与K_ab各向异性的平衡，进而拓宽了Γ₁₂相的稳定范围。

Conclusion: Al掺杂是一种有效调控HoFeO₃中复杂自旋重取向行为和磁相图的手段，为设计具有特定磁相稳定性的稀土正铁氧体材料提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this work, we report the first growth of single crystals of the substitution series HoFe$_{1-x}$Al$_x$O$_3$ with aluminium concentrations up to $x=0.2$ and investigate the evolution of their spontaneous spin-reorientation transition (SRT). Among rare-earth orthoferrites, HoFeO$_3$ exhibits a distinctive sequence of magnetic phases ($Γ_4$-$Γ_{24}$-$Γ_{12}$-$Γ_2$). This complex sequence arises from the competition between the $K_{ac}$ and $K_{ab}$ anisotropies associated with the Ho$^{3+}$ ions and the effective magnetic field produced by the weak ferromagnetic moment of the canted Fe$^{3+}$ sublattice. Introducing magnetic vacancies perturbs the antiferromagnetic compensation of the Fe$^{3+}$ subsystem in the $ab$ plane and generates an additional effective magnetic field acting on the Ho$^{3+}$ ions. This field alters the balance between the $K_{ac}$ and $K_{ab}$ anisotropies within the SRT temperature range and thereby broadens the stability range of the $Γ_{12}$ phase in the magnetic phase diagram.

</details>


### [96] [Optical Response Beyond Magnetic Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16368)
*Javier Sivianes,Enrique Boquete-Someso,Daniel Hernangómez-Pérez,Julen Ibañez-Azpiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出自旋空间群（SSG）作为分析磁体光学响应的更广泛框架，弥补了传统磁空间群（MSG）在非相对论层面的不足，并通过模型与第一性原理计算验证其在线性二色性、双折射及自旋霍尔效应中的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上磁体的光学响应用磁空间群（MSG）分类，但MSG依赖于自旋-轨道耦合锁定自旋与晶格，而多数光学观测量由非相对论物理主导，导致MSG分析可能遗漏关键物理，需要更基础的对称性框架。

Method: 系统发展自旋空间群（SSG）在非相对论层面的变换规则，推导出有效的实空间点群，用于约束电荷响应系数；结合Lieb晶格交替磁体模型调节自旋轨道耦合，并用第一性原理计算UCr2Si2C、RbMnF4及ScMnO3等材料验证。

Result: SSG能给出MSG分析缺失的响应系数关系；在Lieb模型中即使相对论能带劈裂显著，SSG对线性二色性的预测仍精确；UCr2Si2C光学吸收近各向同性，RbMnF4双折射仅限单平面，ScMnO3的自旋霍尔系数层级也由SSG解释。

Conclusion: SSG是比MSG更普适且更具预测力的磁体光学响应分析框架，不仅适用于线性光学，也扩展至自旋电子学相关的自旋霍尔效应，为磁光材料设计提供新工具。

Abstract: The optical response of magnetic materials is conventionally classified through magnetic space groups (MSGs), where spin and lattice are locked by the relativistic spin-orbit interaction. However, most optical observables are governed primarily by nonrelativistic physics, and thus a purely MSG-based description can overlook important insights. Here we systematically show that spin-space groups (SSGs), which operate at the nonrelativistic level, provide a broader and more predictive framework for analyzing a variety of optical responses of magnets. Focusing on linear optical absorption, we derive the transformation rules imposed by SSGs and show that they generate effective real-space point groups, which can enforce relations among charge response coefficients that are absent from conventional MSG analysis. We illustrate the basic principle in a Lieb-lattice altermagnet model with tunable spin-orbit coupling, where SSG predictions on the linear dichroism remain remarkably accurate even when relativistic band splittings become sizable. We further establish the predictive power of this framework through first-principles calculations on two altermagnetic candidates: the actinide UCr2Si2C, where the optical absorption remains nearly isotropic despite its strong spin-orbit coupling and the pronounced anisotropy apparent from magnetic symmetries, and the transition-metal fluoride RbMnF4, where birefringence is confined to a single plane by symmetries emerging exclusively from SSGs. Finally, we extend the concept to the spin Hall response of the coplanar noncollinear antiferromagnet ScMnO3, where SSGs explain the hierarchy of calculated spin Hall coefficients, demonstrating their direct relevance to spintronics as well.

</details>


### [97] [Tuning electronic properties and Schottky contact in graphene-based van der Waals heterostructures by electric gating and interlayer coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16413)
*Poonam Sharma,Archana Sharma,Alok Shukla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于第一性原理计算，系统研究了C6N6/GE、hg-C3N4/GE（半导体/金属）与C6N6/hg-C3N4（半导体/半导体）三类二维范德华异质结的结构、稳定性、电子、力学及光学性质。半导体/金属异质结形成n型肖特基接触，可通过电场或层间耦合调控为p型或欧姆接触；半导体/半导体异质结呈II型能带排列，利于电子-空穴分离，并可在I型与II型间调谐。光学谱显示含石墨烯体系宽频响应，而C6N6/hg-C3N4主要在紫外区活跃；G0W0+BSE计算给出激子结合能分别为1.01、1.14和1.18 eV，表明强激子效应与层间耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯基范德华异质结在理论和实验上具有广泛器件应用潜力，但对其界面电子结构、能带排列及激子行为的系统理解仍不足。本文旨在通过第一性原理方法深入探究不同组合（半导体/金属与半导体/半导体）异质结的物理性质，为设计高性能光电子器件提供理论依据。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（密度泛函理论）系统分析结构稳定性与热力学稳定性、电子结构、力学与光学性质；使用G0W0+BSE方法计算有带隙体系（C6N6、hg-C3N4单层及其异质结）的激子结合能；通过调控垂直电场和层间距来考察能带排列与接触类型的转变。

Result: 半导体/金属（C6N6/GE与hg-C3N4/GE）均为n型肖特基接触，可被电场/层间耦合转换为p型肖特基或欧姆接触；C6N6/hg-C3N4为II型能带排列，且可在电场或层距变化下切换为I型；含GE异质结具有宽频光学响应，而C6N6/hg-C3N4主要在紫外区响应；激子结合能分别为1.01 eV、1.14 eV和1.18 eV，显示强电子-空穴相互作用及明显的层间激子耦合。

Conclusion: 这些二维范德华异质结展现出可调控的接触类型和能带排列，其中C6N6/hg-C3N4的II型排列与强激子效应尤其有利于高效光生载流子分离，为在纳米电子和光电器件中的应用提供了设计指导。

Abstract: Van der Waals heterostructures (vdW HTSs) incorporating graphene (GE) have been an active area of research, both theoretical and experimental, due to their potential to yield devices with a wide variety of applications. In this paper, first-principles calculations are employed to investigate C$_{6}$N$_{6}$/GE, hg-C$_{3}$N$_{4}$/GE, and C$_{6}$N$_{6}$/hg-C$_{3}$N$_{4}$ 2D vdW HTSs. A systematic analysis of structural and thermodynamic stability, electronic, mechanical, and optical properties of semiconductor/metal and semiconductor/semiconductor interfaces is performed. Both semiconductor/metal HTSs form $n$-type Schottky contacts, which can be converted into $p$-type Schottky or Ohmic contacts by tuning the external perpendicular electric field and the interlayer coupling. In the semiconductor/semiconductor C$_{6}$N$_{6}$/hg-C$_{3}$N$_{4}$ HTS, the valence and conduction band edges originate from distinct layers, resulting in a type-II band alignment that promotes efficient electron-hole (e-h) separation. Furthermore, the band alignment can be effectively tuned between type-I and type-II by applying an external electric field and varying the interlayer distance. From the optical absorption spectra of the HTSs, we concluded that the C$_{6}$N$_{6}$/GE and hg-C$_{3}$N$_{4}$/GE exhibit an optical response across a wide frequency range, whereas the C$_{6}$N$_{6}$/hg-C$_{3}$N$_{4}$ HTS shows prominent activity primarily in the ultraviolet region. Using the $G_{0}W_{0}$+BSE approach, the exciton binding energies are also calculated for the gapped systems, namely C$_{6}$N$_{6}$, hg-C$_{3}$N$_{4}$ monolayers, and their HTS (C$_{6}$N$_{6}$/hg-C$_{3}$N$_{4}$), yielding values of 1.01 eV, 1.14 eV, and 1.18 eV, respectively, highlighting strong e-h interactions. Moreover, the band-edge analysis of C$_{6}$N$_{6}$/hg-C$_{3}$N$_{4}$ HTS further favors pronounced interlayer e-h coupling.

</details>


### [98] [Insights into the Structural and Orientational Ordering of 2D Metal Halide Perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16450)
*Mustafa Mahmoud Aboulsaad,German Alvarez Carrero,Trupthi Devaiah Chonamada,Germán Salazar Alvarez,Ute Cappel,Rafael B. Araujo,Tomas Edvinsson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究了2-5单层CsPbBr3纳米片的内部晶格、超晶格组织和薄膜织构，发现正交晶系特征在不同厚度和沉积方式下保持，且厚度决定沉积敏感性：2单层纳米片倾向于面朝上排列，而更厚的纳米片对加工条件敏感且取向无序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属卤化物钙钛矿纳米片兼具量子限域和结构各向异性，但厚度和加工条件如何影响其内部晶格与集体组织尚不明确。

Method: 利用2-5单层CsPbBr3纳米片和较大纳米晶，通过旋涂和滴涂两种方式制备薄膜，采用掠入射X射线衍射（GIXD）分析内部结构、超晶格组织和薄膜织构。

Result: 所有厚度和沉积系列中均出现正交晶系相容特征，晶格-形貌关系保持：020/040晶面与厚度相关，101/202晶面与横向尺寸相关。2单层纳米片在不同沉积方式下均主要呈面朝上取向，而更厚纳米片表现出明显的加工敏感性和取向无序。

Conclusion: 加工可作为调控纳米片堆积的工具，厚度是决定加工敏感性的关键因素。

Abstract: Metal halide perovskite nanoplatelets combine quantum confinement with structural anisotropy, yet how thickness and processing govern their internal crystal lattice versus collective organization remains unresolved. In this study, we separate internal structure, superlattice organization, and film texture using 2-5 monolayer CsPbBr3 nanoplatelets and larger nanocrystals across spin-coating and drop-casting routes. Grazing incidence X-ray diffraction analysis of the specimens indicates that orthorhombic-compatible signatures recur throughout the thickness and deposition series, with a preserved lattice-morphology relationship in which 020/040 and 101/202 lattice planes are associated with the platelet thickness and lateral dimensions, respectively. Upon deposition on the substrate, we notice a thickness-dependent crossover: 2-monolayer nanoplatelets remain predominantly face-on across deposition routes, whereas thicker nanoplatelets show marked processing sensitivity and orientational disorder. Processing therefore can be used as a tool to direct the NPL packing, establishing thickness as a key determinant of processing sensitivity.

</details>


### [99] [Mechanical-microstructural correlation on SPS-fabricated NiTi alloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16511)
*Tadeáš Těhan,Jaromír Kopeček,Elizaveta Iaparova,Eduardo Alarcón,Sneha Samal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究了火花等离子烧结NiTi合金压实样品的力学性能与微观结构，发现低孔隙率样品呈现超弹性，而多孔样品在循环中早期失效，并建立了烧结温度、孔隙率、相变、硬度与力学性能之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粉末冶金中压实工艺对块体材料制备至关重要，但烧结温度如何影响压实演化、微观结构、相变和力学性能的机制尚不明确，需要系统揭示。

Method: 对压实NiTi样品进行力学测试和动态力学分析，结合微观结构观察，考察不同烧结温度下的孔隙率、织构、相变、晶粒尺寸和硬度，并关联力学响应。

Result: 低孔隙率样品表现出超弹性行为，而孔隙率较高的样品在首次循环中即失效，表明孔隙是决定力学响应和失效的关键因素；烧结温度通过调控压实密度和微观结构影响最终性能。

Conclusion: 研究建立了NiTi合金压实样品力学性能与微观结构之间的相关性，为先进工程应用中优化烧结工艺和提升材料可靠性提供了依据。

Abstract: Mechanical properties and dynamical mechanical analysis were performed on compact Spark Plasma Sinter samples. It has been observed that the sample with less porosity reflects the behavior of superelasticity response. Other samples show failure during first cycles that may be due to porosity. Compaction of metallic powder is one of the standard procedures in the field of powder metallurgy for the fabrication of bulk material. Consolidation of the sample as a function of sintering temperature plays a crucial role in the final compaction mechanism. However, the evolution of compaction, microstructure, phase transformation and mechanical properties as a function of sintering temperature is hardly disclosed. In this work, a correlation has been established between mechanical and microstructural properties of compact samples. The maximum compactness and the corresponding microstructure, porosity, texture, phase transformation, grain size, hardness, mechanical properties of compact samples were discussed. The research establishes mechanical properties-structure correlations for compaction of NiTi alloy in advanced engineering applications.

</details>


### [100] [Enhancing Hydrogen Adsorption Ability of MOF-5 with Metal Node Exchange and Linker Functionalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16531)
*Joshua Edzards,Holger-Dietrich Sassnick,Caterina Cocchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算，研究了MOF-5的金属节点等电子取代（Zn→Mg、Cd）与配体官能团化（NH₂、OH、NO₂）对储氢性能的影响，发现Cd取代与NO₂修饰的组合可显著提升H₂吸附能至-15.58 kJ/mol，并揭示其电子结构机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找高性能储氢材料，MOF-5虽具有潜力但吸附能不足，需通过化学修饰系统优化其储氢性能，并理解结构与性能间的内在关联。

Method: 基于第一性原理计算，对MOF-5衍生物进行系统研究：对金属节点进行等电子取代（Zn→Mg、Cd），对配体进行官能团化（NH₂、OH、NO₂），并分析吸附能与电子结构。

Result: 金属节点取代一致地稳定了框架结构，配体官能团化系统增强了金属-氧簇位点的H₂结合能力；Cd与NO₂组合最优，吸附能为-15.58 kJ/mol，显著优于原始MOF-5。

Conclusion: 电子结构分析阐明了局部框架环境对客体分子的配位作用，为实验开发先进吸附材料提供了稳健的设计框架，证明组合调控金属与配体是提升储氢性能的有效策略。

Abstract: In the search for promising material candidates for hydrogen storage, we investigate from first principles derivatives of metal organic framework 5 (MOF-5), including isoelectronic substitution of the metal centers (Zn $\rightarrow$ Mg, Cd) and linker functionalization with NH$_2$, OH, and NO$_2$ groups. Metal-node substitution consistently stabilises and ligand functionalization systematically enhances H$_2$ binding at the metal-oxo cluster sites. The combination of Cd centers and NO$_2$ groups proves to be most efficient, yielding an adsorption energy of -15.58 kJ mol$^{\text{-1}}$, which substantially outperforms the storage ability of pristine MOF-5. Detailed electronic structure analysis clarifies how the local framework environment coordinates the guest molecule, providing a robust design framework to guide the experimental development of advanced adsorbent materials.

</details>


### [101] [Machine Learning-Accelerated Band-Edge Engineering of Pnictogen Chalcohalide Solid Solutions for Solar Energy Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16611)
*Cibrán López,David Rovira,Edgardo Saucedo,Claudio Cazorla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理密度泛函理论与机器学习，系统预测了Bi/Sb硫族卤化物固溶体在两种稳定表面的带边位置，发现组成和表面终止均可显著调控能带，并筛选出可驱动多种电化学半反应及作为空穴选择性接触的特定组分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pnictogen硫族卤化物固溶体具有地球丰产元素、带隙可调（1.2-2.1 eV）和强光吸收等优点，但庞大的组分空间阻碍了对带边位置随化学计量和表面终止如何变化的系统评估。

Method: 结合第一性原理密度泛函理论与机器学习，对Bi_xSb_{1-x}S_ySe_{1-y}I_zBr_{1-z}固溶体在全组分范围内，于(010)和(011)两种最稳定表面的价带和导带带边位置进行预测。

Result: 仅通过组分调控可使价带顶和导带底移动超过1 eV；同一组成在两种表面终止之间带边位移可达0.6 eV，尽管其形成能近乎简并。基于此识别出能驱动氢、氨、甲烷、过氧化氢和氧（光电）电化学半反应的具体组成，并发现常用光伏电子/空穴传输材料与MChX固溶体仅作为空穴选择性接触时能带对齐。

Conclusion: 组分和表面终止均可作为独立设计参数，且表面终止对能带排列的影响可与化学取代相当，为MChX固溶体在太阳能转换中的应用提供了组分筛选和接触层选择的指导。

Abstract: Pnictogen chalcohalide (MChX; M=Bi,Sb; Ch=S,Se; X=I,Br) solid solutions combine earth-abundant constituents, tunable band gaps ($1.2$-$2.1$ eV), and strong optical absorption, making them attractive for solar energy conversion. Yet their vast compositional space has so far prevented a systematic assessment of how band-edge positions vary with stoichiometry and surface termination. Here, we combine first-principles density functional theory with machine learning to predict the valence and conduction band-edge positions of $\mathrm{Bi}_x\mathrm{Sb}_{1-x}\mathrm{S}_y\mathrm{Se}_{1-y}\mathrm{I}_z\mathrm{Br}_{1-z}$ solid solutions across their full compositional range on the two most stable surfaces, (010) and (011). We find that the valence-band maximum and conduction-band minimum can be tuned by more than $1$ eV through composition alone, and shift by up to $0.6$ eV between the two surface terminations for a same composition despite their nearly degenerate formation energies, establishing facet selection as a design parameter on par with chemical substitution. Guided by these results, we identify specific compositions capable of driving hydrogen, ammonia, methane, hydrogen peroxide, and oxygen (photo)electrochemical half-reactions, and show that several electron- and hole-transport contact materials commonly used in photovoltaic devices align with MChX solid solutions only as hole-selective contacts.

</details>


### [102] [Strain control of mid-IR spectroscopic nonlinear photocurrents in PtSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16629)
*M. Gerlei,G. J. de Coster,J. Papp,S. Heiserer,S. Schlosser,G. S. Duesberg,P. Seifert*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在柔性聚酰亚胺衬底上的多晶PtSe2薄膜中，通过单轴拉伸应变实现了对光子拖曳光电流的机械调控，并将其归因于应变通过形变势调节自旋-轨道耦合诱导的带隙，从而为可重构红外偏振探测提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半金属贵金属二硫化物在红外光电子学中很有前景，但其非线性光学响应的起源和可调性尚不清楚，亟需探索有效的调控手段。

Method: 采用偏振分辨中红外光电流光谱，在柔性聚酰亚胺衬底上生长的多晶PtSe2薄膜上施加单轴拉伸应变，并结合理论框架分析光电流的物理机制。

Result: 观察到电阻率、光电导率和螺旋度相关的非线性光电流随应变的显著变化，光谱响应与K点附近自旋-轨道耦合诱导带隙的激发一致，并证明光电流主要由光子动量对称性破缺产生的光子拖曳效应主导，应变通过形变势改变带隙来调控其幅值和偏振依赖。

Conclusion: 机械应变可作为一种有效的调控手段，实现可重构的红外偏振探测。

Abstract: Semi-metallic noble-metal dichalcogenides are promising materials for infrared optoelectronics, yet the origin and tunability of their nonlinear optical responses remain poorly understood. Here, we demonstrate in-situ mechanical control of photon-drag photocurrents in polycrystalline PtSe$_2$ thin films grown on flexible polyimide substrates. Using polarization-resolved mid-infrared photocurrent spectroscopy under uniaxial tensile strain, we observe pronounced strain-dependent changes in resistivity, photoconductivity, and helicity-dependent nonlinear photocurrents. The spectral response is consistent with optical excitations across the spin-orbit-coupling-induced (SOC) band gap near the K point. We develop a theoretical framework attributing the photocurrent primarily to photon-drag induced by photon-momentum symmetry breaking. Strain modifies its magnitude and polarization dependence through deformation potentials that tune the SOC-induced band gap. These results establish mechanical strain as a route toward reconfigurable infrared polarization detection.

</details>


### [103] [Data-driven discovery and rapid, direct synthesis of MXenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16644)
*Ali Saffar Shamshirgar,Guilherme Ribeiro Portugal,Soheil Ershadrad,Roman Ivanov,Martin Dahlqvist,Florian Chabanais,Sanjay Chakraborty,Rainer Traksmaa,Irina Hussainova,Fredrik Heintz,Per O. Å. Persson,Johanna Rosen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过机器学习辅助数据库挖掘与实验结合，从历史文献中发现了38种被忽视的多层MXene候选材料，并利用自蔓延高温合成快速制备了其中5种，进而拓展出11种稀土基M2CT2 MXene，展现出半导体特性和多样的磁状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MXene通常从MAX相获得，但历史报告暗示存在更广泛的未开发化学空间。传统方法限制了对MXene家族多样性的探索，需要一种数据驱动策略来加速发现被忽视的MXene材料。

Method: 采用机器学习辅助的数据库挖掘，筛选已有文献和数据库中的潜在MXene前驱体；结合自蔓延高温合成法，无需持续外部加热，在几分钟内完成反应；通过实验表征和理论计算验证材料性质。

Result: 从数据库中筛选出38种先前已合成但未被认识的ML-MXene候选物；实验合成了5种已知MXene；并进一步制备了11种此前未探索的稀土基M2CT2 MXene（M=Pr, Nd, Sm, Gd, Tb, Ho, Tm），实验和理论均显示其具有半导体行为和多种磁态。

Conclusion: 这些发现扩展了MXene家族，并展示了一种结合数据挖掘与可持续合成方法的材料发现策略，可加速新材料的研发。

Abstract: MXenes, two-dimensional transition-metal carbides and nitrides, are typically obtained from MAX phases, yet historical reports suggest a broader, largely unexplored chemical space. Here we combine machine-learning-assisted database mining with experiments to uncover overlooked multilayer (ml) MXenes. Screening of repositories reveals a "Treasure Chest" of 38 previously synthesized but unrecognized ml-MXene candidates. Guided by these findings, we rediscover five MXenes using a rapid, scalable self-propagating high-temperature synthesis that requires no sustained external heating and completes within minutes. Inspired by the identified chemistries, we further realize 11 previously unexplored rare-earth-based M2CT2 MXenes (M= Pr, Nd, Sm, Gd, Tb, Ho, and Tm). Experiments and theory reveal semiconducting behavior and diverse magnetic states across this family. Together, these results expand the MXene family and demonstrate a data-driven strategy for accelerating materials discovery through sustainable methods.

</details>


### [104] [A nuclear-quantum-corrected machine-learning potential reveals quantum-enhanced hydrogen segregation at general grain boundaries in alpha-iron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16652)
*Kazuma Ito*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过用路径积分分子动力学在300K下计算的量子平均力重新标记Fe-H机器学习势（PACE）的训练构型，构建了核量子效应修正的NQC-PACE势，无需额外DFT计算即可描述氢在α-Fe中的扩散、缺陷捕获及晶界偏聚等行为。巨正则蒙特卡洛/分子动力学模拟表明核量子效应显著增强氢在一般晶界的偏聚，且捕获行为更接近实验趋势，其机制源于对开放、各向异性软局域环境的选择性量子稳定化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢脆的原子级理解需要描述氢在缺陷处的扩散与捕获，而氢作为最轻溶质，即使在室温下其核量子效应也会改变这些过程。然而显式处理核量子效应的计算成本极高，限制了对复杂环境的大规模模拟。

Method: 采用基于原子团簇展开（PACE）的高效Fe-H机器学习势，利用离心约束路径积分分子动力学在300K下产生的量子平均力，对支撑势函数迁移性的训练构型进行重新标记，从而得到核量子效应修正的NQC-PACE势，无需额外进行密度泛函理论计算。随后执行巨正则蒙特卡洛/分子动力学模拟分析氢的行为。

Result: NQC-PACE成功描述了核量子效应对氢在空位、位错、表面和一般晶界处的捕获、H-H相互作用及在α-Fe中扩散的影响。巨正则模拟显示核量子效应显著增强氢在一般晶界的偏聚，并使捕获行为与实验趋势更一致；这种增强源于对开放且各向异性较软的局域环境的选择性量子稳定化。

Conclusion: 该框架利用有限温度量子平均力重新标记机器学习势所覆盖的构型空间，使得在复杂材料的大规模模拟中能够有效纳入轻元素核量子效应，为研究氢脆等与量子效应相关的材料问题提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Atomistic descriptions of hydrogen diffusion and trapping at defects are essential for understanding hydrogen embrittlement. As the lightest solute in metals, hydrogen exhibits nuclear quantum effects that alter these processes even at room temperature. Explicit treatment of such effects is computationally demanding, limiting large-scale simulations of complex environments. Here, we use an Fe-H machine-learning interatomic potential (MLIP) based on the performant implementation of the atomic cluster expansion (PACE), covering diverse Fe-H environments, and relabel the training configurations underpinning its transferability with quantum mean forces from centroid-constrained path-integral molecular dynamics at 300 K. This yields a nuclear-quantum-corrected PACE (NQC-PACE) without additional density functional theory calculations. At parent PACE, NQC-PACE describes nuclear quantum effects on hydrogen trapping at vacancies, dislocations, surfaces and general grain boundaries, H-H interactions, and diffusion in alpha-Fe. Grand-canonical Monte Carlo/molecular dynamics simulations show nuclear quantum effects markedly enhance hydrogen segregation at general grain boundaries and trapping behaviour in closer agreement with experimental trends. This enhancement arises from selective quantum stabilisation of open, anisotropically soft local environments. Our framework uses finite-temperature quantum mean forces to relabel the configurational space covered by an MLIP, enabling large-scale analysis of complex materials where light-element quantum effects matter.

</details>


### [105] [Automated Burgers Vector Identification for Individual Dislocations in Bulk Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16774)
*Abderrahmane Benhadjira,Carsten Detlefs,Vincent Favre-Nicolin,Henning Friis Poulsen,Grethe Winther,Can Yildirim,Sina Borgi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理信息卷积神经网络（CNN）的暗场X射线显微镜（DFXM）位错Burgers矢量自动识别方法，通过几何光学模拟训练并在合成与实验数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱束成像DFXM可分辨块体晶体中的单个位错，但从衬度中确定Burgers矢量通常需要手动与正向模拟对比，过程繁琐且依赖经验，亟需自动化识别方法。

Method: 利用面心立方（FCC）铝中孤立位错的几何光学模拟数据训练物理信息CNN，将晶体学约束融入学习过程，从弱束积分摇摆曲线图像中识别Burgers矢量。

Result: 在合成测试集上准确率约93%；在实验交叉滑移案例中，约束模型将72.7%的逐层预测归因于参考Burgers矢量。

Conclusion: 研究表明，经模拟训练的物理信息CNN可推动DFXM中位错识别的自动化，为实验数据的高效、可靠分析提供可行途径。

Abstract: Weak-beam imaging in dark-field X-ray microscopy (DFXM) can resolve individual dislocations in bulk crystals, but assigning Burgers vectors from the resulting contrast typically requires manual comparison with forward simulations. Here, we train a physics-informed convolutional neural network (CNN) on geometrical optics simulations of isolated dislocations in face-centred cubic (FCC) aluminium, incorporating crystallographic constraints into the learning pro- cess. The model identifies Burgers vectors from weak-beam integrated rocking-curve images. On synthetic test data, the model achieves an accuracy of approximately 93%. In an experimental cross-slip case, the constrained model as- signs 72.7% of the layer-wise predictions to the reference Burgers vector. These results show that simulation-trained, physics-informed CNNs represent a step toward automated dislocation identification in DFXM.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

### [106] [Electronic structure and magnetic correlations in the epitaxially strained bilayer nickelate La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_{7}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14810)
*I. V. Leonov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 使用DFT+动力学平均场方法研究了双层Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐La3Ni2O7中电子关联和外延应变对正常态电子结构、准粒子带重整化、费米面和磁关联的影响，发现轨道选择性重整化和非相干性，以及应变对磁关联和超导性的显著调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解La3Ni2O7中电子关联与晶格应变如何共同影响其电子结构和磁性质，特别是轨道选择性局域化倾向以及费米面嵌套驱动的自旋/电荷密度波不稳定性，为压力或应变调控超导性提供理论依据。

Method: 采用DFT+动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）方法，系统计算不同外延应变条件下La3Ni2O7的电子结构、准粒子重整化、费米面拓扑及磁关联，并分析轨道分辨的Ni 3d态行为。

Result: 发现Ni 3d带呈现显著的轨道选择性重整化和强非相干性，接近x²-y²和3z²-r²态的轨道选择性局域化；面内应变强烈影响电子性质，拉伸和中等压缩（约-2%）应变增强磁关联，而大压缩（约-4%）应变导致Lifshitz转变，γ费米面消失，伴随键合Ni 3z²-r²轨道近全占的浅平带，磁关联急剧下降，暗示超导被抑制。

Conclusion: 结果支持La3Ni2O7中由费米面嵌套驱动的自旋和电荷密度波条纹不稳定性，并表明压力和应变可有效调控自旋/电荷密度波有序，从而增强自旋涨落，为超导性调控提供途径。

Abstract: Using the DFT+dynamical mean-field theory method we study the effects of electron-electron correlations and epitaxial strain of the crystal structure on the normal-state electronic structure, quasiparticle band renormalizations, Fermi surface, and magnetic correlations of the bilayer Ruddlesden-Popper nickelate La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ (LNO). Our results exhibit a remarkable orbital-selective renormalization and strong incoherence of the Ni $3d$ bands, pointing to the proximity of the Ni $x^2-y^2$ and $3z^2-r^2$ states to orbital-selective localization. The electronic properties of LNO show a high sensitivity to the in-plane strain. We note that both a tensile and a moderate compressive strain (up to about $-2$\%) yield a significant enhancement of magnetic correlations compared to the unstrained LNO. Under a large compressive strain of about $-4$\%, we observe a Lifshitz transition characterized by the disappearance of the $γ$ Fermi surface sheet, which is associated with a nearly fully occupied, shallow flat-band of the bonding Ni $3z^2-r^2$ orbital character. As a result, we observe a sharp decrease of magnetic correlations, implying suppression of superconductivity. Overall, our results support the picture of spin- and change-density-wave stripe instability driven by the Fermi surface nesting in LNO. Our results suggest that both pressure and strain can effectively tune (suppress or enhance) spin-change-density-wave ordering, giving rise to enhanced spin fluctuations.

</details>


### [107] [Antiferroquadrupolar Order in Altermagnetic CoF$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14889)
*Daniel Halliday,Laura Pöysti,Chung Xu,Daniel A. Mayoh,Didier Wermeille,Dharmalingam Prabhakaran,David R. Bowler,Roger D. Johnson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过共振弹性X射线散射、对称性分析和从头计算，实验证实CoF₂中存在反铁四极子电荷序，并证明该电荷序是d波交变磁性自旋劈裂的微观起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体的非相对论自旋劈裂电子态理论上与隐藏电荷序相关，但此前缺乏直接的实验研究，因此本文旨在实验探测CoF₂中的电荷序。

Method: 结合共振弹性X射线散射、对称性分析和从头算电子结构计算，直接观测CoF₂的电荷有序态并确定其对称性，进而计算该电荷序对电子结构的影响。

Result: 直接观察到CoF₂中的电荷序，并识别为反铁四极子类型；电子结构计算表明该反铁四极子序产生了特征性的交变磁自旋劈裂，建立了交变磁序参量分解为磁和电荷自由度的实验证据。

Conclusion: 反铁四极子序是CoF₂中交变磁性的微观起源，并可能推广至更广泛的金红石交变磁体；共振弹性X射线散射可作为探测交变磁体中电荷多极子的直接手段。

Abstract: Altermagnets host non-relativistic spin-split electronic states whose microscopic origin is theoretically linked to a hidden charge order, yet experimental studies of this charge order are limited. We therefore investigate charge order within CoF$_2$, a $d$-wave altermagnetic compound with a rutile crystal structure and $Γ$-point antiferromagnetism. Combining resonant elastic X-ray scattering, symmetry analysis and ab initio calculations, we directly observe charge ordering and identify it as antiferroquadrupolar in nature. Via electronic structure calculations, we show that the experimentally observed antiferroquadrupolar order gives rise to the characteristic altermagnetic spin-splitting, thereby establishing empirical evidence for the decomposition of the altermagnetic order parameter into magnetic and charge degrees of freedom. We hence demonstrate that antiferroquadrupolar order is the microscopic origin of altermagnetism in CoF$_2$, with implications to the wider family of rutile altermagnets. Furthermore, our approach is applicable to studying altermagnetism in general, having demonstrated that resonant elastic X-ray scattering can serve as a direct probe of the charge multipoles that underpin spin-split electronic states in these materials.

</details>


### [108] [Low temperature magnetic structure and lattice response in SmCuAs$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14920)
*M. G. Kim,C. Neupane,Y. Yu,R. Acevedo-Esteves,C. Nelson,D. Evans,E. D. Mun,D. F. Agterberg,J. -W. Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过高分辨同步辐射X射线衍射和Sm L2/L3边X射线共振磁散射，研究了单晶SmCuAs2的结构与磁性。结果表明其在8 K至室温保持四方结构，在电阻率极小值(~30 K)以下出现晶格反常，并在Néel温度附近c轴出现平台和增大，反映磁弹耦合。磁结构为传播矢量q=(0,0,0.5)的共线反铁磁序，Sm磁矩位于ab面内，沿c轴呈++--堆叠。与Pr、Nd、Gd同系物对比，面内磁矩取向和磁阻挫是电阻率极小值出现的关键，而自旋轨道耦合和磁弹耦合差异调控低温输运行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解SmCuAs2的结构、磁性与低温输运异常（如电阻率极小值）之间的关联，并澄清其磁结构及与同系物RECuAs2的对比关系。

Method: 使用高分辨率同步辐射X射线衍射测量温度依赖的晶体结构，使用Sm L2和L3边的X射线共振磁散射确定磁有序的传播矢量、磁矩取向和堆叠方式，并与RE = Pr、Nd、Gd的文献结果进行比较。

Result: SmCuAs2在整个温区保持四方对称；晶格参数在T≈30 K以下出现反常，c轴在Néel温度附近出现平台和增大，表明存在磁弹耦合。XRMS证实反铁磁序为q=(0,0,0.5)的 commensurate 结构，Sm磁矩在ab面内，沿c轴为++--排列。

Conclusion: SmCuAs2的电阻率极小值源于面内磁矩取向和相关的磁阻挫；不同RE体系间自旋轨道耦合和磁弹耦合的差异对低温输运行为起关键作用。

Abstract: We investigated the structural and magnetic properties of single-crystalline SmCuAs$_2$ using high-resolution synchrotron X-ray diffraction and X-ray resonant magnetic scattering (XRMS) at the Sm $L_2$ and $L_3$ edges. Temperature-dependent diffraction measurements confirm that SmCuAs$_2$ maintains its tetragonal symmetry from room temperature down to 8 K, with lattice parameters showing anomalous behavior below the resistivity minimum ($T \approx$ 30 K). Notably, the \textbf{c}-axis lattice parameter exhibits a plateau and subsequent increase near the Néel temperature, indicating magnetoelastic coupling. XRMS measurements reveal a commensurate antiferromagnetic structure with a propagation vector \textbf{\textit{q}} = (0, 0, 0.5). Our measurement shows that the Sm moments are aligned within the \textbf{\textit{ab}} plane and arranged in a $++--$ stacking along the \textbf{\textit{c}}-axis. Comparison with related \textit{RE}CuAs$_2$ compounds (\textit{RE} = Pr, Nd, and Gd) suggests that in-plane moment orientation and associated magnetic frustration play a key role in the emergence of the resistivity minimum. Differences in spin-orbit and magnetoelastic coupling across the series highlight their importance in governing low-temperature transport behavior.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum Many-Body Scars, Magnon-Pair Condensation, and Hilbert Space Fragmentation in an Anisotropic Heisenberg Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15017)
*J. Y. Liu-Sun,Z. Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了由两个相同二分格点构成的晶格上的自旋-1/2各向异性海森堡模型，发现受限谱生成代数（RSGA）产生的精确本征态构成量子多体疤痕，这些疤痕是磁振子对凝聚体，具有非广延纠缠熵和离域长程序；在共振点可精确映射为混合自旋-1和自旋-0的XY模型，导致希尔伯特空间碎裂，从而实现疤痕、磁振子对凝聚与碎裂的自然共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子多体疤痕（QMBT）与希尔伯特空间碎裂（HSF）等非平衡现象之间的内在联系，并希望在一个可精确求解的模型中同时实现这些现象，以加深对遍历性破坏和量子动力学行为的理解。

Method: 基于自旋-1/2各向异性海森堡模型，利用受限谱生成代数构造精确本征态；通过调节子晶格间相互作用到共振点，将模型映射为混合自旋-1/自旋-0 XY模型，并将哈密顿量分解为按自旋构型标记的独立子哈密顿量；分析自旋-0粒子的动力学约束及其导致的希尔伯特空间碎裂。

Result: RSGA生成的一族精确本征态表现为磁振子对凝聚体，具有亚广延纠缠熵和离域长程序；共振点映射后的模型中，自旋-0粒子作为动力学约束隔离了相邻格点，使得子空间彼此独立，呈现希尔伯特空间碎裂；这些现象在统一框架下自然共存。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个精确可解的平台，其中量子多体疤痕、具有离域长程序的磁振子对凝聚以及希尔伯特空间碎裂可以天然共存，为研究非平衡量子物质和遍历性破缺提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We investigate a spin-$1/2$ anisotropic Heisenberg model on a lattice consisting of two identical bipartite sublattices. A family of exact eigenstates generated by the restricted spectrum generating algebra (RSGA) constitutes quantum many-body scar states, characterized by subextensive entanglement entropy and supporting. These scar states are magnon-pair condensates exhibiting off-diagonal long-range order (ODLRO). At the resonance point of the inter-sublattice interaction, the model exactly maps onto a mixed spin-$1$ and spin-$0$ XY model on a bipartite lattice, which decomposes into independent sub-Hamiltonians labeled by all possible spin configurations. Each spin-$0$ particle is dynamically isolated from its neighbors and acts as a kinetic constraint, giving rise to emergent Hilbert space fragmentation (HSF). Our work establishes an exactly solvable platform in which quantum many-body scars, magnon-pair condensation exhibiting off-diagonal long-range order, and Hilbert space fragmentation naturally coexist.

</details>


### [110] [Zero-point theorems in quantum many-body physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15542)
*Yuan Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出基于谱隙不可避免零点的零点型论证，并将其推广到多参数哈密顿族；仅依赖参数边界的模型无关变换关系，即可对任意高维量子自旋模型临界现象的可能相关算符给出普适约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统零点定理只考虑单参数哈密顿族，不足以覆盖更一般的量子自旋系统相图；同时希望在不必指定低能动力学或响应的情况下，仅利用模型无关的结构来约束临界现象。

Method: 考虑多参数哈密顿族，类比零点定理，沿参数边界施加模型无关的变换关系，论证谱隙零点必然存在；并进一步提出一系列统一推广相关结论的猜想。

Result: 得到了对任意高维量子自旋模型中临界现象相关算符的强大且普适的模型无关约束，涵盖多种维度与多参数情形。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了更一般的零点型论证框架，为量子自旋系统相图和临界行为提供了基于拓扑/变换关系的普适限制，并可通过猜想进一步推广。

Abstract: We propose several zero-point type arguments based on the inevitable zero point(s) of a spectral gap in the quantum spin system phase diagrams in various dimensions. We consider multi-parameter families of Hamiltonian extending the conventional zero-point theorem that includes only one parameter. Analogously to the zero-point theorem, we only impose model-independent transformation relations along the parameter boundary, rather than specifying any low-energy dynamics or response. We further give a series of conjectures, which generalize our statements in a uniform way. Our results give powerful and universal model-independent constraints on the possible relevant operators for critical phenomena in quantum spin models in arbitrary high dimensions.

</details>


### [111] [Accessing Gapped Chiral Phase with Auxiliary-Assisted PEPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15732)
*Sen Niu,Rui-Zhen Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种辅助辅助框架，通过将手性物理系统与其时间反演辅助系统嵌入为非手性扩大表示，使有限键维PEPS能无伪长程关联地描述二维手性带隙相，并保持手性拓扑纠缠信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限键维PEPS在描述二维手性带隙相时虽能捕捉局域和拓扑性质，但会引入虚假的长程幂律关联，导致转移矩阵关联长度发散，因此直接表示手性纯态存在根本性困难。

Method: 引入辅助时间反演伙伴，将物理手性系统与非手性扩大表示耦合，通过受控退耦恢复物理手性扇区；对自由费米Chern绝缘体和相互作用手性自旋液体构造PEPS，并使用层分辨动量投影提取纠缠边界谱。

Result: 该方案得到干净的带隙关联函数和有限转移矩阵关联长度，消除了直接手性PEPS的人工长程尾巴；物理与辅助系统间仅存在由有限纠缠引起的微小可忽略耦合，且手性拓扑信息仍编码在纠缠中，恢复出预期的普适手性纠缠边界谱。

Conclusion: 通过改变表示问题而非直接研究手性纯态，该框架为用有限键维PEPS访问二维手性带隙相提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: It has been controversial whether infinite projected entangled pair state (PEPS) can faithfully describe chiral gapped phases in two dimensions or not. Finite-bond-dimension PEPS can capture many local and topological properties of chiral phases, but generically develop spurious long-range power-law like correlations. We introduce an auxiliary-assisted framework that bypass this obstruction by embedding the physical chiral system together with an auxiliary time-reversed partner, yielding a non-chiral enlarged representation whose physical chiral sector is recovered with controlled decoupling. For both a free-fermion Chern insulator and an interacting chiral spin liquid, the resulting PEPS show clean gapped correlation functions, and a finite transfer-matrix correlation length, in contrast with the artificial long-range tail of direct chiral PEPS representations. There exist small and negligibly coupling between the physical and auxiliary systems due to the finite entanglement effect, which only affect short-ranged local quantities. Despite the nonchiral enlarged representation, the chiral topological information remains encoded in the entanglement. Using layer-resolved momentum projection, we recover the expected universal chiral entanglement boundary spectrum. Our study provides a practical route to access gapped chiral phases with finite bond-dimension PEPS by changing the representation problem rather than directly studying the chiral pure state.

</details>


### [112] [Scarred discrete time crystal in a periodically driven dimerized spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16616)
*Davood Marripour,Saeed S. Jahromi,Jahanfar Abouie*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在周期性驱动的二聚化自旋链中发现了疤痕离散时间晶体（SDTC）相，该相由量子多体疤痕诱导的弱遍历性破缺所支持，呈现周期倍增的亚谐波振荡和离散时间平移对称性自发破缺，但最终因疤痕与热连续谱杂化而寿命饱和，属于长寿命亚稳态动力学现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索周期性驱动相互作用系统中除热化之外的例外行为，特别是量子多体疤痕能否在无无序系统中产生弱遍历性破缺，并理解周期性驱动与约束多体动力学的相互作用。

Method: 利用精确对角化方法，通过分析Floquet能级统计、纠缠熵和本征态保真度来识别低纠缠疤痕态，并测试不同初始态以及进行有限尺寸标度分析。

Result: 在热谱中识别出具有半泊松统计的低纠缠疤痕态，这些态支持稳健的周期倍增亚谐波振荡；时间晶体寿命在可计算的尺寸范围内随系统尺寸增长，但预测在更大尺寸下会因疤痕与热连续谱的杂化而饱和。

Conclusion: SDTC应被刻画为长寿命亚稳态动力学区域而非严格稳定的热力学相，为理解无无序系统中周期性驱动与约束多体动力学的相互作用提供了综合框架。

Abstract: We investigate the emergence of a scarred discrete time crystal (SDTC) phase in a periodically driven dimerized spin chain. While generic interacting Floquet systems are expected to thermalize according to the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH), we demonstrate that this system hosts quantum many-body scars (QMBS) that induce a regime of weak ergodicity breaking. Through an analysis of Floquet level statistics, entanglement entropy, and eigenstate fidelity, we identify a manifold of low-entanglement states characterized by semi-Poisson statistics embedded within an otherwise thermal spectrum. These scarred states support robust subharmonic oscillations with period doubling, signaling the spontaneous breaking of discrete time-translation symmetry. We show that the SDTC response is robust against a variety of initial state configurations, demonstrating its stability beyond fine-tuned conditions. A finite-size scaling analysis reveals that the time-crystalline lifetime grows with system size within the range accessible to our exact-diagonalization calculations. However, drawing on the general phenomenology of approximate many-body scars, we expect that hybridization between Floquet scars and the thermal continuum will eventually curtail this growth, causing the lifetime to saturate at system sizes beyond our current numerical reach. This characterizes the SDTC as a long-lived metastable dynamical regime rather than a strictly stable thermodynamic phase, providing a comprehensive framework for understanding the interplay between periodic driving and constrained many-body dynamics in disorder-free systems.

</details>


### [113] [Linear response functions from inhomogeneous dynamical mean-field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15800)
*Don Rolih,Fabian B. Kugler*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种基于非均匀动力学平均场理论计算静态晶格磁化率的方法，通过外部场响应绕过双粒子顶点函数，并以数值重整化群为杂质求解器，在方形晶格Hubbard模型中验证其与标准顶点方法结果一致，且能达到低温。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算静态晶格响应需要求双粒子顶点函数，数值上困难且难以进入低温区域；需要一种避开顶点函数、兼容多种杂质求解器的新方法。

Method: 在非均匀DMFT框架下，施加外部场并测量系统响应，从中提取静态晶格磁化率，从而完全绕过双粒子顶点函数的计算。

Result: 针对方形晶格Hubbard模型的磁化率计算，结果与标准顶点方法吻合良好，并能有效到达其他方法难以触及的低温。

Conclusion: 该方法因避免顶点函数且兼容几乎任何杂质求解器，为低温下静态磁化率等晶格响应的高效计算提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We present a method for calculating static lattice susceptibilities with inhomogeneous dynamical mean-field theory. The method utilizes the response of the system to an external field and thereby circumvents two-particle vertex functions. We demonstrate the success of our approach for the magnetic susceptibility of the square-lattice Hubbard model using the numerical renormalization group as an impurity solver and show that it compares well with the results obtained using the standard vertex approach. As it avoids vertex functions and is compatible with virtually any impurity solver, our method is able to reach low temperatures that are hard to access with other methods.

</details>


### [114] [Topological Altermagnetic Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15811)
*Jasmin Bedow,Nitin Kaushal,Marcel Franz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究Ising自旋轨道耦合下交替磁拓扑相的出现，在Lieb格子相关交替磁体中，通过Hartree-Fock与精确对角化发现量子自旋霍尔效应与交替磁自旋序在宽参数区域共存，并证明边缘磁结构影响拓扑边缘模且对反演对称破缺具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索交替磁体中自旋轨道耦合与电子关联共同作用下的拓扑相，理解边缘磁构型对拓扑边缘态的影响及其稳定性。

Method: 采用Hartree-Fock近似和精确对角化技术处理电子关联，在Lieb格子模型中分析平均电子密度为2和4每原胞时的相图与边缘态性质。

Result: 在宽参数区域内，量子自旋霍尔效应与交替磁自旋序共存；边缘磁结构调控拓扑边缘模行为，且这些边缘模对反演对称破缺项表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 交替磁体与Ising自旋轨道耦合可稳定拓扑相位，边缘磁性为调控拓扑边缘态提供新自由度，且系统对反演对称破缺具有耐受性。

Abstract: We study the emergence of altermagnetic topological phases stabilized by Ising spin-orbit coupling in a prototypical Lieb lattice model of a correlated altermagnet. Treating the electron interactions within the Hartree-Fock and the exact diagonalization techniques, we establish the co-existence of a quantum spin Hall effect with altermagnetic spin order over a wide region of the parameter space for average electron densities of 2 and 4 per unit cell. We explore how the magnetic structure along edges influences the electronic behavior of the associated topological edge modes, demonstrating their robustness against inversion-symmetry breaking terms.

</details>


### [115] [Transport-Noise Witnesses of Electronic Multipartite Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16028)
*Shuhan Ding,Prakash Sharma,Zecheng Shen,Jiang-Xiazi Lin,Sergei Urazhdin,Yao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种基于输运的纠缠见证协议，利用平衡噪声谱来认证多体电子系统中的多分纠缠，并在多种强关联模型中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠是强关联量子材料区别于常规金属和半导体的核心特征，认证固体中电子间的本征纠缠对量子态分类和量子技术发展至关重要，但常规光谱手段在超低温下难以适用。

Method: 从微观模型参数出发，推导对称化、投影后的电流噪声在适当积分下满足一个与电子基选择无关的上界，以此作为纠缠判据；该协议以平衡噪声谱为实验可观测物理量，无需散射测量。

Result: 在扭曲双层石墨烯、扭曲双层MoTe₂和Hubbard模型中验证了该框架，成功认证了分数量子霍尔绝缘态中的纠缠，表明该方法能覆盖多种强关联范例系统。

Conclusion: 该输运纠缠见证方案将基于散射的纠缠见证推广到超低温材料体系，填补了传统谱学手段无法适用的空白，为候选纠缠态的实验认证提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Entanglement among particles is a defining feature of strongly correlated quantum materials, distinguishing them from conventional metals and semiconductors. The ability to certify intrinsic entanglement among interacting electrons in solid-state materials is important not only for classifying quantum states of matter, but also for developing material-based quantum technologies. Here, we introduce a transport-based protocol for witnessing multipartite entangled electronic states, based on the equilibrium noise spectrum as an experimentally accessible observable. The appropriately integrated, symmetrized, and projected current noise obeys an upper bound that can be derived from microscopic model parameters and is invariant with respect to the choice of electronic basis. We benchmark this framework in several paradigmatic systems, including twisted bilayer graphene, twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$, and Hubbard models, certifying entanglement in the fractional Chern insulating state. The method extends recently developed scattering-based entanglement witnesses to ultralow-temperature materials, where conventional spectroscopic probes are inaccessible but candidate entangled states are expected to arise.

</details>


### [116] [Non-Equilibrium Instantaneous Approximation and Dipole Forbidden d-d Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16219)
*Marco Marino,Lasse Sternemann,Mirko Cinchetti,Frithjof B. Anders*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种用于泵浦驱动关联电子系统的瞬时近似方法，结合单次动力学平均场（Hubbard I）与超胞展开，可在保持动量分辨的同时高效计算时间分辨光电子谱，并成功复现FePS3中d-d激发后的主要实验特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间分辨角分辨光电子谱能直接探测泵浦诱导的电子结构变化，但其理论描述通常需要计算量巨大的双时间非平衡模拟。为降低计算成本并保留物理本质，需要发展一种近似框架，能在相对时间快衰减与平均时间慢演化分离的基础上高效处理关联材料中的泵浦动力学。

Method: 引入基于格林函数相对时间快衰减与平均时间慢演化的瞬时近似，并与Hubbard I层次的单次动力学平均场构造结合，将局域关联壳层的驱动动力学纳入晶格格林函数并保留动量分辨率。同时推导了名义上偶极禁戒的d-d激发的有效耦合，其来源于偶极允许的d-p跃迁后接p-d杂化，并在瞬时极限外得到能量依赖的顶点修正。

Result: 以密度泛函理论导出的紧束缚模型结合超胞展开方法，对顺磁和反铁磁FePS3进行计算，得到的动量分辨谱成功再现了近期时间分辨光电子谱实验中激发第一、第二d-d跃迁后观察到的主要定性特征。

Conclusion: 该框架为高效模拟强关联材料中的泵浦诱导电子动力学提供了一条可行路径，能够捕捉动量分辨的瞬态谱学特征，且为进一步包含非瞬时顶点修正的光学响应计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: Time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy provides direct access to pump-induced changes in the electronic structure of correlated materials, but its theoretical description generally requires computationally demanding two-time non-equilibrium calculations. We introduce an instantaneous approximation for pump-driven correlated systems, based on a separation between the rapid decay of the Green's functions in the relative time variable and their slower evolution in the average time. Combined with a one-shot dynamical mean-field construction at the Hubbard I level, the method incorporates the driven dynamics of the local correlated shell into the lattice Green's function while retaining momentum resolution. We also derive an effective coupling for nominally dipole-forbidden d-d excitations. It arises from a dipole-allowed d-p transition followed by p-d hybridization; beyond the instantaneous limit, the same process produces an energy-dependent vertex correction to the optical response. As a proof of principle, we apply the framework to paramagnetic and antiferromagnetic FePS3, using a density-functional theory derived tight-binding model together with a supercell unfolding procedure. The calculated momentum-resolved spectra reproduce the main qualitative features observed after excitation of the first and second d-d transitions in recent time-resolved photoemission experiments.

</details>


### [117] [Non-invertible Lattice 1-Form Symmetries for Non-Abelian Topological Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16520)
*Rafael Flores-Calderón,Frank Pollmann,Michael Knap*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文针对有限非阿贝尔群 G 的量子双格点模型 D(G)，在格点不动点上构造了电、磁与 dyonic 1-形式对称算子，证明它们构成完整的非局域诊断代数；利用该代数显式确定了任意有限 G 的柱面与环面基态子空间，并计算了 1-形式对称性的微观融合与胶合，表明其拓扑形变性质在投影到无缺陷拓扑子空间后涌现。这些结果将非阿贝尔量子双模型的基态确立为自发非可逆 1-形式对称性破缺的具体微观实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高阶形式对称性通常与阿贝尔拓扑相相关，其 1-形式对称性构成群；而非阿贝尔拓扑相基于有限群时，需要由非可逆融合代数描述的 1-形式对称算子。然而在晶格模型层面，这类非可逆 1-形式对称性的精确构造和系统诊断工具此前仍然缺乏，这限制了从对称性角度理解非阿贝尔拓扑序。

Method: 在量子双格点模型 D(G) 的格点不动点上，直接构造电、磁及 dyonic 1-形式算子，并证明它们组合成完整的非局域诊断代数。利用该代数对拓扑希尔伯特空间进行刻画，显式计算柱面和环面上的基态子空间；随后研究 1-形式对称算子的微观融合规则与胶合过程，并分析投影至无缺陷拓扑子空间后所得的拓扑形变性质。

Result: 对任意有限非阿贝尔群 G，得到了柱面与环面基态子空间的明确表达式；构造的 1-形式对称算子在微观层面满足非可逆融合与胶合关系，且其拓扑性质仅在投影到无缺陷拓扑子空间后显现。这证明了非阿贝尔量子双模型基态是自发非可逆 1-形式对称性破缺的微观实例，并为刻画此类拓扑态提供了算子语言。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了非阿贝尔拓扑相中非可逆 1-形式对称性的精确格点实现，深化了对自发非可逆高阶对称性破缺的理解；所提出的非局域诊断算子有望为量子处理器上非阿贝尔拓扑态的制备与表征提供实用工具。

Abstract: Higher-form symmetries generalize conventional global symmetries and act on lower-dimensional submanifolds of a quantum system. While Abelian topological phases can be organized by 1-form symmetries that form a group, non-Abelian topological phases based on finite groups require 1-form symmetry operators governed by non-invertible fusion algebras. In this work, we make this statement precise in quantum double lattice models $\mathcal D(G)$ for finite non-Abelian groups $G$. We construct the electric, magnetic, and dyonic 1-form operators directly at the lattice fixed point and show that together they form a complete nonlocal diagnostic algebra for the topological Hilbert space. Using these operators, we explicitly determine the cylinder and torus ground-state subspaces for arbitrary finite $G$. Furthermore, we calculate the microscopic fusion and gluing of the 1-form symmetries and show that their topological deformation properties emerge after projection to the defect-free topological subspace. Our results establish ground states of non-Abelian quantum double models as a concrete microscopic realization of spontaneous non-invertible 1-form symmetry breaking and provide an operator language that may be useful for characterizing such states in quantum processors.

</details>


### [118] [Resolving the Magnetic Ground State and Field-Induced Transitions in Magnetic Dirac Semimetal Candidate EuMnSb$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16724)
*Yiu-Fung Chiu,Jian-Rui Soh,Sanjay Sharma,J. Alberto Rodríguez-Velamazán,John Singleton,Eugen Weschke,Oleksandr Prokhnenko,Oksana Zaharko,Dharmalingam Prabhakaran,Stephen J. Blundell,Paul A. Goddard,Andrew T. Boothroyd*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过中子衍射、X射线磁圆二色和磁强计等技术，研究了磁性拓扑半金属EuMnSb₂在高达30 T磁场下的磁结构，发现低场磁转变仅涉及Eu自旋重排而Mn自旋序不受影响，并在Eu饱和场附近观察到额外转变；结合平均场模型估算了主导的Eu–Eu与Eu–Mn交换相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解磁性拓扑半金属中磁性序与电子拓扑的耦合机制，需要精确表征Eu和Mn两个子晶格的磁结构及其对外加磁场的响应，尤其是揭示低场与高场下磁相变的微观起源。

Method: 综合使用偏振和非偏振中子衍射、脉冲磁场X射线磁圆二色以及脉冲磁场磁强计测量，在最高30 T的磁场下研究磁结构；并构建平均场模型来模拟关键磁性行为，提取交换相互作用参数。

Result: 确定了零场下Eu和Mn子晶格的磁结构；2 T以下的磁场诱导相变完全由Eu自旋排列改变引起，Mn自旋序无明显扰动；在接近Eu自旋饱和场处出现了额外的磁转变；平均场模型成功再现主要磁性特征并量化了Eu–Eu和Eu–Mn交换相互作用。

Conclusion: 该工作厘清了EuMnSb₂中不同磁场区间的磁序演化，证明Eu子系统主导低能磁响应，并提供了Eu–Eu及Eu–Mn交换作用的定量估计，为理解磁序与拓扑电子态的耦合提供了关键实验依据。

Abstract: The magnetic structure of a magnetic topological semimetal EuMnSb$_2$ is investigated in fields up to 30 T using polarized and unpolarized neutron diffraction, pulsed-field x-ray magnetic circular dichroism and pulsed-field magnetometry. We determine the zero-field magnetic structures of the Eu and Mn sublattices, and find that magnetic transitions induced by applied fields below 2 T correspond to changes in the magnetic order of the Eu spins alone without detectable perturbation to the order of the Mn spins. An additional magnetic transition is observed at fields close to the saturation field for the Eu spins. We present a mean-field model which describes key features of the magnetic behavior and allows us to estimate the dominant Eu--Eu and Eu--Mn exchange interactions responsible for the coupling between magnetism and electronic topology.

</details>

### [119] [Tracking Chirality during Molecular Motor Photoisomerization via Simulated Time-Resolved Circular Dichroism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15330)
*Leonardo Biancorosso,Ali Hassanali,Mauro Stener,Emanuele Coccia,Marta Monti,Gonzalo Díaz Mirón*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 提出一种沿非绝热分子动力学系综模拟时间分辨电子圆二色谱(TRCD)的框架，并用于第二代分子马达光异构化，发现TRCD可区分P/M异构体分支，而瞬态吸收无法区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快光谱技术广泛用于研究光诱导过程，但传统方法对分子手性几乎不敏感；需要发展能实时探测光反应中立体化学分支的手性敏感光谱方法。

Method: 建立TRCD模拟框架，将其与非绝热分子动力学系综结合，对第二代分子马达的光异构化进行计算，比较瞬态吸收与TRCD响应。

Result: 瞬态吸收仅能反映整体激发态动力学，无法区分两条光产物路径；TRCD则可分辨立体化学分支：返回稳定P异构体(右手螺旋)的轨迹在可见区保留明显手性光谱带，而形成M异构体(左手螺旋)的轨迹在通过圆锥交叉扭曲时手性信号变暗。

Conclusion: TRCD的不对称性可作为立体化学分支的直接可测特征，为实时探测分子手性形成提供新途径，并为未来TRCD实验提供具体预测。

Abstract: Ultrafast spectroscopic techniques are widely used to investigate photoinduced processes, yet they remain largely blind to molecular chirality. Here we introduce a framework for simulating time-resolved electronic circular dichroism (TRCD) along an ensemble of nonadiabatic molecular dynamics, and apply it to the photoisomerization of a second-generation molecular motor[1]. While transient absorption captures the overall excited-state dynamics, it cannot distinguish the two photoproduct pathways. The TRCD response, by contrast, resolves the stereochemical branching: trajectories returning to the stable P isomer (right-handed helix) retain a distinct chiroptical band in the visible region, whereas those forming the M isomer (leftl-handed helix) become chiroptically dark as they twist through the conical intersection. This asymmetry constitutes a directly measurable signature of the stereochemical branching, offering a real-time probe of the formation of molecular chirality and concrete predictions for future TRCD experiments.
